b) if he or she commits such an act as a person who has a specially imposed duty to defend the interests of the injured party,
c) if he or she commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening the life or health of people, public order or property, or
d) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,
a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he or she commits such an act with the intention of enabling or facilitating the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, the criminal offence of financing terrorism (Section 312d) or threatening a terrorist criminal offence (Section 312f). Unauthorised use of another's thing
(1) Whoever takes possession of another's thing of not small value or a motor vehicle with the intention of using it temporarily, or
whoever causes damage not small to another's property by temporarily using such things that were entrusted to him or her without authorisation,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or prohibition of activity,
a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a person who has a specially imposed duty to defend the interests of the injured party,
b) if he or she commits such an act as a member of an organised group, or
c) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years or a fine,
a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he or she commits such an act with the intention of enabling or facilitating the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, the criminal offence of financing terrorism (Section 312d) or threatening a terrorist criminal offence (Section 312f). Unauthorised interference with the right to a house, flat or non-residential premises
(1) Whoever unlawfully occupies or uses another's house, flat or non-residential premises shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or a fine. (2) Whoever unlawfully prevents an entitled person from using a house, flat or non-residential premises shall be punished in the same manner. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or a fine,
a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group, or
b) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by such an act. Fraud
(1) Whoever enriches himself or herself or another by misleading someone, taking advantage of someone's error or concealing essential facts, and thereby causes damage not insignificant to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and was convicted or punished for such an act in the last three years. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years or a fine if he or she causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years,
a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he or she commits such an act as a person who has a specially imposed duty to defend the interests of the injured party,
c) if he or she commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening the life or health of people, public order or property, or
d) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,
a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he or she commits such an act with the intention of enabling or facilitating the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, the criminal offence of financing terrorism (Section 312d) or threatening a terrorist criminal offence (Section 312f). Insurance fraud
(1) Whoever states false or grossly distorted information or conceals essential information
a) in connection with the conclusion or amendment of an insurance contract,
b) in connection with the settlement of an insured event, or
c) when asserting the right to performance from insurance or other similar performance,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) Whoever with the intention of procuring for himself or herself or for another a benefit causes or simulates an event with which the right to performance from insurance or other similar performance is connected, or maintains a state caused by an insured event, and thereby causes damage not insignificant to another's property, shall be punished in the same manner. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and was convicted or punished for such an act in the last three years. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years or a fine if he or she causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years,
a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he or she commits such an act as a person who has a specially imposed duty to defend the interests of the injured party, or
c) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act. (6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,
a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) if he or she commits such an act with the intention of enabling or facilitating the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, the criminal offence of financing terrorism (Section 312d) or threatening a terrorist criminal offence (Section 312f). Credit fraud
(1) Whoever states false or grossly distorted information or conceals essential information when negotiating a credit agreement or when drawing down credit shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever without the consent of the creditor, to a not small extent, uses funds obtained by a purpose-specific credit for a purpose other than the designated one shall be punished in the same manner. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and was convicted or punished for such an act in the last three years. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years or a fine if he or she causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years,
a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he or she commits such an act as a person who has a specially imposed duty to defend the interests of the injured party, or
c) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act. (6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,
a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) if he or she commits such an act with the intention of enabling or facilitating the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, the criminal offence of financing terrorism (Section 312d) or threatening a terrorist criminal offence (Section 312f). Subsidy fraud
(1) Whoever states false or grossly distorted information or conceals essential information in an application for the provision of a subsidy, grant or repayable financial assistance or contribution shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever uses, to a not small extent, funds obtained by a purpose-specific subsidy, grant or repayable financial assistance or contribution for a purpose other than the designated one shall be punished in the same manner. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and was convicted or punished for such an act in the last three years. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years or a fine if he or she causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years,
a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he or she commits such an act as a person who has a specially imposed duty to defend the interests of the injured party, or
c) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act. (6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,
a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) if he or she commits such an act with the intention of enabling or facilitating the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, the criminal offence of financing terrorism (Section 312d) or threatening a terrorist criminal offence (Section 312f). Operating dishonest games and bets
(1) Whoever operates a monetary or other similar game or bet whose rules do not guarantee equal chances of winning to all participants shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and was convicted or punished for such an act in the last three years. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years or a fine,
a) if he or she causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he or she obtains for himself or herself or for another a greater benefit by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years,
a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act, or
c) if he or she obtains for himself or herself or for another a substantial benefit by such an act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,
a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he or she obtains for himself or herself or for another a benefit on a large scale by such an act.

## § 214, § 215 repealed

Money Laundering
(1) Whoever conceals, transfers to himself or another, harbours or uses a thing which is proceeds from a criminal offence committed in the territory of the Czech Republic or abroad by another person, or
whoever converts such a thing with the intent to enable another person to escape criminal prosecution, punishment or protective measure or their execution, or
whoever conspires to commit such a criminal offence,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years, by a fine, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) Whoever disguises the origin of a thing which is proceeds from a criminal offence committed in the territory of the Czech Republic or abroad, in particular by concealing or hiding its true nature, location, movement, disposition thereof, ownership or other right thereto, or whoever otherwise endeavours to substantially hinder or prevent the determination of its origin, or
whoever conspires to commit such a criminal offence,
shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to six years or by a fine,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in relation to a thing originating from a felony,
b) if he commits such an act in relation to a thing which has greater value, or
c) if he obtains for himself or another greater benefit by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he commits such an act in relation to a thing originating from a particularly serious felony,
c) if he commits such an act in relation to a thing which has substantial value,
d) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act, or
e) if he commits such an act because he breached an important duty arising from his employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him by law. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in connection with an organised group operating in more than one state,
b) if he commits such an act in relation to a thing which has value on a large scale, or
c) if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by such an act. Special Provision on Punishment
In determining the type of punishment and its measure for the criminal offence of money laundering pursuant to § 216(1) and (2), the court shall also take into account the level of the penalty rate established for the criminal offence from which the proceeds from criminal activity originate, if the Criminal Code establishes a milder punishment for that criminal offence. Money Laundering by Negligence
(1) Whoever by negligence enables another to disguise the origin or determination of the origin of a thing of greater value which is proceeds from a criminal offence committed in the territory of the Czech Republic or abroad, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever by negligence conceals, transfers to himself or another, harbours or uses a thing of greater value which is proceeds from a criminal offence committed in the territory of the Czech Republic or abroad by another person. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 because he breached an important duty arising from his employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him by law,
b) if he commits such an act in relation to a thing originating from a felony,
c) if he commits such an act in relation to a thing which has substantial value, or
d) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in relation to a thing originating from a particularly serious felony,
b) if he commits such an act in relation to a thing which has value on a large scale, or
c) if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by such an act. Common Provision
For the purposes of § 216 and 217, criminal activity committed abroad means an act committed abroad which fulfils the elements of a criminal offence under the law of the Czech Republic, regardless of whether it is also punishable under the law of the state in whose territory it was committed. Usury
(1) Whoever, by exploiting someone's mental weakness, distress, inexperience, recklessness or someone's agitation, causes himself or another to be provided with or promised performance the value of which is grossly disproportionate to the value of the reciprocal performance, or
whoever asserts such a claim or transfers it to himself with the intent to assert it,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,
a) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) if he commits such an act as a member of an organised group, or
c) if he causes another a state of severe need by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening the life or health of people, public order or property, or
b) if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by such an act. Concealment of a Thing
(1) Whoever appropriates another's thing of not insignificant value which has come into his possession by finding, by mistake or otherwise without the consent of the person entitled, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Breach of Duty in the Administration of Another's Property
(1) Whoever breaches a duty imposed on him by law or contractually assumed to safeguard or administer another's property, and thereby causes not minor damage to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a person who has a specially imposed duty to defend the interests of the injured party, or
b) if he causes substantial damage by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Breach of Duty in the Administration of Another's Property by Negligence
(1) Whoever by gross negligence breaches an important duty imposed on him by law or contractually assumed in safeguarding or administering another's property, and thereby causes substantial damage to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a person who has a specially imposed duty to defend the interests of the injured party, or
b) if he causes damage on a large scale by such an act. Damaging a Creditor
(1) Whoever, even if only partially, frustrates the satisfaction of his creditor by
a) destroying, damaging, concealing, alienating, rendering unusable, or removing, even if only part of his property,
b) assigning his claim, or assuming another's debt,
c) encumbering a thing which is the subject of an obligation, or leasing it,
d) simulating or acknowledging a non-existent right or obligation,
e) simulating or acknowledging a right or obligation to a greater extent than corresponds to reality,
f) simulating the fulfilment of an obligation, or
g) simulating insolvency or otherwise ostensibly reducing his property or simulating its extinction,
and thereby causes not minor damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, even if only partially, frustrates the satisfaction of a creditor of another person by
a) destroying, damaging, concealing, alienating, rendering unusable or removing, even if only part of the debtor's property, or
b) asserting a non-existent right or claim to the debtor's property or an existing right or claim in a higher value or better ranking than it has,
and thereby causes not minor damage to another's property. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years,
a) if he causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years,
a) if he causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2,
b) if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by such an act, or
c) if he causes another's insolvency by such an act. Preferring a Creditor
(1) Whoever as a debtor who is insolvent, even if only partially, frustrates the satisfaction of his creditor by preferring another creditor, and thereby causes not minor damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years,
a) if he causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he causes another's insolvency by such an act. Causing Insolvency
(1) Whoever, even if by gross negligence, brings about his own insolvency by
a) making expenditures grossly disproportionate to his property circumstances,
b) administering his property in a manner which does not correspond to duties imposed on him by law or contractually assumed or is grossly disproportionate to them,
c) using a provided credit in contravention or gross disproportion to its purpose,
d) providing loans or credits from his property to other persons, although this is grossly disproportionate to his property circumstances, or
e) making, beyond the scope of ordinary business risk, a transaction or operation which does not belong to his regular business activity or is grossly disproportionate to his property circumstances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, even if by gross negligence, accepts a new obligation or establishes a pledge, although he knows that he is insolvent, and thereby worsens the position of existing creditors. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years if he causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. Breach of Duty in Insolvency Proceedings
Whoever in insolvency proceedings frustrates or grossly hinders the performance of the function of the insolvency administrator, and thereby endangers the purpose of the insolvency proceedings, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activity. Machinations in Insolvency Proceedings
(1) Whoever as a creditor in connection with voting of creditors in insolvency proceedings accepts or has promised to him in contravention of the principles and rules of insolvency proceedings pecuniary or other benefit, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever provides, offers or promises to a creditor in connection with voting of creditors in insolvency proceedings in contravention of the principles and rules of insolvency proceedings pecuniary or other benefit. (3) Whoever as an insolvency administrator, member of the creditors' committee or representative of creditors in insolvency proceedings accepts or has promised to him for himself or another to the detriment of creditors pecuniary or other benefit which is not due to him, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years,
a) if he causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3,
b) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act, or
c) if he commits such an act as an official person. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to six years,
a) if he causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3, or
b) if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by such an act. Breach of Duty to Make a Truthful Declaration of Property
Whoever in proceedings before a court or other public authority refuses to fulfil the statutory duty to make a declaration of property or evades such duty or states false or grossly distorted information in such a declaration, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, by a fine or by prohibition of activity. Damage to Another's Thing
(1) Whoever destroys, damages or renders unusable another's thing, and thereby causes not insignificant damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever damages another's thing by spraying, painting or inscribing it with paint or another substance. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 on a thing of a witness, expert or interpreter for the performance of their duty,
b) if he commits such an act on another's thing for his actual or supposed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion,
c) if he commits such an act on a thing which enjoys protection under another legal regulation, or
d) if he causes substantial damage by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to six years if he causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. Abuse of Ownership
Whoever damages an important interest of culture, science, protection of nature, landscape or the environment, protected by another legal regulation, by destroying, damaging, rendering unusable or squandering his own thing of greater value which enjoys protection under another legal regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. Unauthorised Access to a Computer System and Unauthorised Interference with a Computer System or Information Carrier
(1) Whoever overcomes a security measure, and thereby gains unauthorised access to a computer system or part thereof, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) Whoever interferes with a computer system or information carrier by
a) unauthorisedly using data stored in a computer system or on an information carrier,
b) unauthorisedly deleting or otherwise destroying, damaging, altering, suppressing, reducing the quality of or rendering unusable data stored in a computer system or on an information carrier,
c) forging or altering data stored in a computer system or on an information carrier so that it would be considered genuine or acted upon as if it were genuine data, regardless of whether such data is directly readable and comprehensible, or
d) unauthorisedly inserting or transferring data into a computer system or onto an information carrier or making other interference with the software or technical equipment of a computer system or other technical device for data processing,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2
a) with the intent to cause damage or other detriment to another or to obtain unauthorised benefit for himself or another, or
b) with the intent to unauthorisedly restrict the functionality of a computer system or other technical device for data processing. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years or by a fine,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he causes substantial damage by such an act,
c) if he commits such an act against a computer system whose disruption would have a serious impact on the functioning of the state, health of persons, security, economy or provision of basic living needs of the population,
d) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act, or
e) if he causes a serious disruption in the activity of a legal person or natural person who is an entrepreneur by such an act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years,
a) if he causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by such an act. Procurement and Harbouring of Access Device and Password to a Computer System and Other Such Data
(1) Whoever manufactures, puts into circulation, imports, exports, transports, offers, intermediates, sells or otherwise makes accessible, procures for himself or another or harbours
a) a device or its component, procedure, tool or any other means, including a computer program, created or adapted for unauthorised access to an electronic communications network, to a computer system or part thereof or for unauthorised interference with a computer system or information carrier, or
b) a computer password, access code, data, procedure or any other similar means by which access to a computer system or part thereof can be obtained,
with the intent that it be used to commit the criminal offence of breach of secrecy of transmitted messages pursuant to § 182(1)(b) or (c) or the criminal offence of unauthorised access to a computer system and unauthorised interference with a computer system or information carrier pursuant to § 230(1) or (2), shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by forfeiture of a thing or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group, or
b) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Unauthorised Interference with a Computer System or Information Carrier by Negligence
(1) Whoever by gross negligence in breach of a duty arising from employment, profession, position or function or imposed by law or contractually assumed
a) destroys, damages, alters or renders unusable data stored in a computer system or on an information carrier, or
b) makes interference with the technical or software equipment of a computer system or other technical device for data processing,
and thereby causes substantial damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing if he causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. ECONOMIC CRIMINAL OFFENCES
Criminal Offences against Currency and Means of Payment
Counterfeiting and Alteration of Money
(1) Whoever procures for himself or another or harbours counterfeit or altered money or elements of money serving to protect against their counterfeiting, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. (2) Whoever counterfeits or alters money with the intent to pass it off as genuine or valid or as money of higher value, or
whoever passes off counterfeit or altered money as genuine or valid or as money of higher value,
shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group, or
b) if he commits such an act on a substantial scale. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group operating in more than one state, or
b) if he commits such an act on a large scale. Unauthorised Procurement, Counterfeiting and Alteration of a Means of Payment
(1) Whoever procures for himself or another, makes accessible, accepts or harbours without the consent of the authorised user a means of payment which enables the withdrawal of cash or transfer of monetary funds or virtual assets used instead of monetary funds (hereinafter "means of payment") and which belongs to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) Whoever procures for himself or another, makes accessible, accepts or harbours a counterfeit or altered means of payment, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. (3) Whoever counterfeits or alters a means of payment with the intent to use it as genuine or valid, or
whoever uses a counterfeit or altered means of payment as genuine or valid,
shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 as a member of an organised group, or
b) if he commits such an act on a substantial scale. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 as a member of an organised group operating in more than one state, or
b) if he commits such an act on a large scale. Passing Off Counterfeit and Altered Money
Whoever passes off as genuine counterfeit or altered money with which he was paid as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. Manufacture and Possession of Counterfeiting Tools and Equipment Intended for Unauthorised Procurement of a Means of Payment
(1) Whoever manufactures, puts into circulation, imports, exports, transports, offers, sells, intermediates or otherwise makes accessible, procures for himself or another or harbours a tool, device or its component, procedure, aid or any other means, including a computer program, created or adapted for counterfeiting or alteration of money or elements serving to protect money against counterfeiting or created or adapted for counterfeiting, alteration or unauthorised procurement of a means of payment, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years or by a fine if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in the exercise of his profession. Unauthorised Manufacture of Money
(1) Whoever unauthorisedly, using equipment or materials intended for the manufacture of money and held in accordance with the law, manufactures money or elements serving to protect money against counterfeiting, or
whoever procures for himself or another, puts into circulation or harbours unauthorisedly manufactured money or elements serving to protect money against counterfeiting,
shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group, or
b) if he commits such an act on a substantial scale. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group operating in more than one state, or
b) if he commits such an act on a large scale. Common Provision
Protection pursuant to § 233 to 237 is also provided to money and means of payment other than domestic and to domestic and foreign securities. Endangering the Circulation of Domestic Money
(1) Whoever unauthorisedly manufactures or issues substitutes for domestic money, or
whoever unauthorisedly puts such substitutes into circulation,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever
a) refuses domestic money without legal grounds, or
Tax, Fee and Foreign Exchange Criminal Offences
Reduction of Tax, Fee and Similar Compulsory Payment
(1) Whoever on a larger scale reduces tax, customs duty, social security insurance premium, contribution to state employment policy, accident insurance premium, health insurance premium, fee or other similar compulsory payment or obtains an advantage on any of these compulsory payments, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with at least two persons,
b) if he breaks an official seal to facilitate such an act, or
c) if he commits such an act on a substantial scale. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale, or
b) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 2(c) in connection with an organised group operating in more than one state. Failure to Remit Tax, Social Security Insurance Premium and Similar Compulsory Payment
(1) Whoever on a larger scale fails to fulfil as an employer or payer his statutory duty to remit for an employee or other person tax, social security insurance premium, contribution to state employment policy or health insurance premium, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years or by a fine if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Special Provision on Effective Remorse
Criminal liability for the criminal offence of failure to remit tax, social security insurance premium and similar compulsory payment (§ 241) ceases if the offender subsequently fulfilled his duty before the court of first instance began to pronounce the judgment. Failure to Fulfil Notification Duty in Tax Proceedings
(1) Whoever fails to fulfil his statutory notification duty towards the tax administrator, and thereby endangers on a larger scale the proper and timely determination of tax for another or its collection from another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to four years or by a fine if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale. Breach of Regulations on Stamps and Other Items for Marking Goods
(1) Whoever handles stamps, control strips or other items for marking goods for tax purposes in contravention of another legal regulation with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain unauthorised benefit for himself or another, or
whoever in contravention of another legal regulation imports, stores, transports or puts into circulation goods without stamps, control strips or other items for marking it for tax purposes,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with at least two persons, or
b) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Counterfeiting and Alteration of Items for Marking Goods for Tax Purposes and Items Evidencing Fulfilment of Fee Obligation
(1) Whoever counterfeits or alters stamps, control strips or other items for marking goods for tax purposes or items issued by a public authority or a legal person authorised by it as proof of fulfilment of fee obligation with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain unauthorised benefit for himself or another, or
whoever puts such stamps, strips or items into circulation or uses them as genuine,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale, or
b) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Counterfeiting and Alteration of Stamps
(1) Whoever counterfeits or alters postage or revenue stamps with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain unauthorised benefit for himself or another, or
whoever intentionally puts such stamps into circulation or uses them as genuine,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale, or
b) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Endangering Foreign Exchange Management
Whoever during a state of emergency breaches a prohibition imposed by a measure issued on the basis of the crisis act in relation to foreign exchange management and thereby causes serious endangerment of payment capacity towards abroad or internal monetary balance of the Czech Republic, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to six years, by a fine or by prohibition of activity. Criminal Offences against Binding Rules of Market Economy and Circulation of Goods in Relations with Abroad
Breach of Regulations on Rules of Economic Competition
(1) Whoever breaches another legal regulation on unfair competition by committing in participation in economic competition
b) misleading designation of goods and services,
c) causing danger of confusion,
d) parasitising on the reputation of an enterprise, products or services of another competitor,
h) breach of trade secret, or
i) endangering the health of consumers and the environment,
and thereby causes detriment on a larger scale to other competitors or consumers or thereby obtains for himself or another unauthorised advantages on a larger scale, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. whoever in contravention of another legal regulation on protection of economic competition concludes with his competitor an agreement on price determination, an agreement on market division or another agreement disrupting economic competition,
whoever in contravention of another legal regulation on public procurement breaches in a serious manner binding rules of tender procedure, or
whoever in contravention of another legal regulation regulating the activity of persons authorised to do business in the financial market, the activity of banks and other legal persons authorised to conduct financial activity, trading in investment instruments, pension insurance and insurance, management and administration of investment funds or foreign investment funds, breaches in a serious manner binding rules of prudent business, property management, professional care or the prohibition to perform acts, services or other activities determined by law or official decision,
and thereby causes detriment on a larger scale to other competitors or consumers, to the contracting authority or another supplier or thereby obtains for himself or another unauthorised advantages on a larger scale. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he commits such an act repeatedly,
c) if he causes substantial damage by such an act, or
d) if he obtains for himself or another substantial benefit by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years,
a) if he causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2,
b) if he obtains for himself or another benefit on a large scale by such an act, or
c) if he causes another's insolvency by such an act. Special Provision on Effective Remorse
Criminal liability for the criminal offence of breach of regulations on rules of economic competition pursuant to § 248(2) first paragraph ceases if the offender fulfils the conditions under another legal regulation on protection of economic competition for refraining from imposing a fine or for reducing a fine for participation in an agreement on price determination, an agreement on market division or another agreement disrupting economic competition with his competitor. Unauthorised Issue of a Security
(1) Whoever with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain unauthorised advantage or benefit for himself or another puts into circulation as a security a document without the conditions established by law for its issue being fulfilled, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever with the intent referred to in paragraph 1 has an entry made on the issue of a book-entry security in the evidence of securities established by law, without the conditions established by law for such entry being fulfilled.Manipulation of the Price of Investment Instruments
(1) Whoever, with the intent to influence the price or rate of investment instruments that are admitted to trading in a trading system or for whose admission to trading in a trading system an application has been made,
a) disseminates or provides to another false or grossly distorted information significantly affecting the price or rate of such investment instruments or related commodities traded on an organised commodity market, or the calculation of rates, indices or quantitatively expressed indicators determining the price or rate of such investment instruments, or
b) executes a transaction, gives an order or engages in other conduct capable of creating a false impression about the supply, demand, price or rate of such investment instrument or related commodities traded on an organised commodity market, or about the value of a rate, index or quantitatively expressed indicator determining the price or rate of such investment instruments,
shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he obtains considerable benefit by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he obtains large-scale benefit by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Unauthorised Business Activity
(1) Whoever unlawfully provides services or carries on production, commercial or other business activity on a larger scale shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,
a) if he causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,
a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act. Unauthorised Operation of a Game of Chance
(1) Whoever unlawfully operates, organises, promotes or intermediates a game of chance shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group, or
b) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Harming a Consumer
(1) Whoever causes not insignificant damage to another's property by harming consumers, in particular by deceiving them as to the quality, quantity or weight of goods, or
whoever places on the market on a larger scale products, works or services and conceals their substantial defects,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years or by a fine,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such act, or
c) if he has been convicted of such an act in the last five years or released from imprisonment imposed for such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Distortion of Data on the State of Economic Activity and Assets
(1) Whoever fails to keep accounting books, records or other documents serving to provide an overview of the state of economic activity and property or to control them, although he is obliged to do so by law,
whoever enters false or grossly distorted data in such accounting books, records or other documents, or
whoever alters, destroys, damages, renders unusable or conceals such accounting books, records or other documents,
and thereby endangers the property rights of another or the timely and proper assessment of tax, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on
whoever enters false or grossly distorted data in documents serving for entry in the commercial register, foundation register, register of public benefit corporations or register of unit owners' associations, or conceals material facts in such documents,
whoever enters false or grossly distorted data in documents serving for the preparation of an expert opinion that is attached to an application for entry in the commercial register, foundation register, register of public benefit corporations or register of unit owners' associations, or conceals material data in such documents, or
whoever endangers or restricts another in his rights by failing without undue delay to file an application for entry of data required by law in the commercial register, foundation register, register of public benefit corporations or register of unit owners' associations, or to deposit a document in the collection of documents, although he is obliged to do so by law or contract. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if he causes substantial damage to another's property by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he causes large-scale damage to another's property by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. Misuse of Information in Commercial Relations
(1) Whoever unlawfully publishes, makes accessible or communicates to a third party information not yet publicly accessible, the publication of which is capable of substantially influencing decision-making in commercial relations, and thereby causes greater damage or other serious consequence or thereby procures greater benefit for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever unlawfully uses information not yet publicly accessible, which he obtained in the course of his employment, profession, position, function or otherwise and the publication of which is capable of substantially influencing decision-making in commercial relations, by executing or giving an impetus to execute a contract or operation in a trading system or on an organised commodity market, and thereby causes greater damage or other serious consequence or thereby procures greater benefit for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years or by prohibition of activity. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,
a) if he causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,
a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act. Abuse of Position in Commercial Relations
(1) Whoever, as an entrepreneur, partner, member of a body, employee or participant in the business of two or more entrepreneurs with the same or similar subject of activity, with the intent to procure an advantage or benefit for himself or another, concludes or gives an impetus to conclude a contract to the detriment of one or more entrepreneurs or their enterprises, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Procuring an Advantage in the Award of a Public Contract, in a Public Competition and Public Auction
(1) Whoever, in connection with the award of a public contract, a public competition or public auction, with the intent to cause damage to another or to procure benefit for himself or another, procures for a supplier, competitor or auction participant an advantage or more favourable conditions at the expense of other suppliers or competitors, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an evaluation committee, announcer or organiser of a public competition or public auction, auctioneer or as a member of an organised group,
b) he causes substantial damage by such act, or
c) he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such act. (3) The same punishment as in paragraph 2 shall be imposed on whoever, under the circumstances referred to in paragraph 1, requests, accepts or has promised to him property or other benefit. (4) Whoever, under the circumstances referred to in paragraph 1, requests, accepts or has promised to him property or other benefit and commits such act as an official person, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years. Machinations in the Award of a Public Contract and in a Public Competition
(1) Whoever engages in machinations in connection with the award of a public contract or a public competition by
a) compelling another by deceit or threat of violence or other serious harm to refrain from participating in award proceedings or in a public competition,
b) providing, offering or promising to another property or other benefit for refraining from participating in award proceedings or in a public competition,
c) requesting or accepting property or other benefit for refraining from participating in award proceedings or in a public competition, or
d) engaging, on the basis of an agreement with another interested party or tenderer, in activity aimed at awarding a public contract at an unreasonably high or otherwise disadvantageous price,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) with the intent to procure considerable benefit for himself or another, or
b) as a member of an organised group. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) with the intent to procure large-scale benefit for himself or another, or
b) as an official person with the intent to procure considerable benefit for himself or another. Machinations in a Public Auction
(1) Whoever engages in machinations in a public auction of a thing by
a) compelling another by deceit or threat of violence or other serious harm to refrain from participating in submitting bids at the auction,
b) providing, offering or promising to another property or other benefit for refraining from submitting bids at the auction, or
c) requesting or accepting property or other benefit for refraining from submitting bids at the auction,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) with the intent to procure considerable benefit for himself or another, or
b) as a member of an organised group. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) with the intent to procure large-scale benefit for himself or another, or
b) as an official person with the intent to procure considerable benefit for himself or another. Issuance of False Certificates and Reports
Whoever, in the name of a bank or other entrepreneur authorised to carry on financial activities under another legal regulation, issues to another a false certificate about his financial situation or his property circumstances, or
whoever, as an auditor, issues to another a false auditor's report or false certificate about the financial situation or property circumstances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. Damaging the Financial Interests of the European Union
(1) Whoever prepares, uses or presents false, incorrect or incomplete documents, provides false, incorrect or incomplete data or conceals documents or data, and thereby enables unauthorised use or retention of financial resources originating from the budget of the European Union or budgets managed by the European Union or on its behalf, or reduction of the resources of any such budget, or enables unauthorised use or retention of property acquired from the budget of the European Union or budgets managed by the European Union or on its behalf, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever unlawfully uses financial resources originating from the budget of the European Union or budgets managed by the European Union or on its behalf, property acquired from the budget of the European Union or budgets managed by the European Union or on its behalf, or reduces the resources of any such budget. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he commits such act as a person who has a specially imposed duty to protect the interests of the European Union, or
c) if he causes substantial damage by such act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. Breach of Regulations on the Circulation of Goods in Relations with Foreign Countries
(1) Whoever substantially endangers the general interest by breaching a prohibition, restriction or other important obligation established for the import, export or transit of goods shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with at least two persons,
b) if he causes substantial damage by such act, or
c) if he causes or increases by such act the danger of introduction or spread of an infectious disease of animals in hobby breeding, farm animals or wild animals, or an infectious disease or pest of useful plants. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,
a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he causes or increases by such act the danger of introduction or spread of an infectious human disease. Breach of Regulations on Export Control of Dual-Use Goods and Technologies
Whoever exports dual-use goods or technologies without a valid permit shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years, by a fine or by forfeiture of property. Breach of Obligations in the Export of Dual-Use Goods and Technologies
(1) Whoever breaches or fails to fulfil an important obligation arising from his employment, profession, position or function, and thereby causes a permit for the export of dual-use goods or technologies to be unlawfully issued or such goods to escape from records, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,
a) if, as a result of the act referred to in paragraph 1, the goods were exported,
b) if he commits such act with the intent to obtain considerable benefit for himself or another, or
c) if he causes substantial damage by such act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in connection with an organised group operating in several states,
b) if he commits such act with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another, or
c) if he causes large-scale damage by such act. Distortion of Data and Failure to Keep Records Regarding the Export of Dual-Use Goods and Technologies
(1) Whoever obtains the issuance of a permit for the export of dual-use goods or technologies on the basis of false or incomplete data shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever destroys, damages, renders unusable, conceals the documents necessary for the records of the export of dual-use goods and technologies or fails to keep records, or whoever makes an intervention in the technical or software equipment of a computer system in which records of such goods and technologies are kept. Conducting Foreign Trade in Military Material Without a Permit or Licence
(1) Whoever conducts foreign trade in military material without a permit or licence shall be punished by imprisonment for one to eight years, by a fine or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in connection with an organised group,
b) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war,
c) if he commits such act repeatedly,
d) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such act,
e) if he causes large-scale damage by such act,
f) if he commits such act in relation to significant military material, or
g) if he causes by such act that military material reaches an area of war or other armed conflict or a state where there is an increased danger of war or other armed conflict. Breach of Obligation in Connection with the Issuance of a Permit and Licence for Foreign Trade in Military Material
(1) Whoever breaches or fails to fulfil an important obligation arising from his employment, profession, position or function, and thereby causes a permit for conducting foreign trade in military material or a licence for a specific trade in military material to be unlawfully issued, or issues a false or incomplete document on the basis of which such permit or licence is unlawfully issued, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years or by a fine,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in connection with an organised group,
b) if he causes by such act that military material reached a foreign country,
c) if he commits such act with the intent to obtain considerable benefit for himself or another,
d) if he causes substantial damage by such act, or
e) if he commits such act in relation to significant military material. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in connection with an organised group operating in several states,
b) if he commits such act with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another,
c) if he causes large-scale damage by such act, or
d) if he causes by such act that military material reaches an area of war or other armed conflict or a state where there is an increased danger of war or other armed conflict. Distortion of Data and Failure to Keep Records Regarding Foreign Trade in Military Material
(1) Whoever supports an application for a permit or licence for foreign trade in military material with a false or incomplete document or conceals facts important for the issuance of a permit or licence shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on
whoever destroys, damages, renders unusable, conceals the documents necessary for the records of foreign trade in military material or fails to keep records, or
whoever makes an intervention in the technical or software equipment of a computer system in which records of foreign trade in military material are kept. Criminal Offences Against Industrial Rights and Copyright
Infringement of Rights to a Trademark and Other Designations
(1) Whoever places in circulation products or provides services unlawfully marked with a trademark to which another has an exclusive right, or with a mark confusingly similar thereto, or for this purpose offers, intermediates, manufactures, imports, exports or otherwise procures or stores such products for himself or another, or offers or intermediates such service, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, for the purpose of achieving economic benefit, unlawfully uses a business name or any designation confusingly similar thereto, or places in circulation products or services unlawfully bearing a designation of origin or geographical indication or a designation confusingly similar thereto, or for this purpose offers, intermediates, manufactures, imports, exports or otherwise procures or stores such products or services for himself or another. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing,
a) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) if he commits such act on a substantial scale. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,
a) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) if he commits such act on a large scale. Infringement of Protected Industrial Rights
(1) Whoever unlawfully interferes not insignificantly with rights to a protected invention, industrial design, utility model or topography of a semiconductor product shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing,
a) if the act referred to in paragraph 1 exhibits the characteristics of commercial activity or other business,
b) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such act, or
c) if he commits such act on a substantial scale. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,
a) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he commits such act on a large scale. Infringement of Copyright, Rights Related to Copyright and Rights to a Database
(1) Whoever unlawfully interferes not insignificantly with legally protected rights to a copyrighted work, artistic performance, sound or audiovisual recording, radio or television broadcast, press publication or database shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing,
a) if the act referred to in paragraph 1 exhibits the characteristics of commercial activity or other business,
b) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another or causes substantial damage to another by such act, or
c) if he commits such act on a substantial scale. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,
a) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another or causes large-scale damage to another by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he commits such act on a large scale. Forgery and Imitation of a Work of Fine Art
(1) Whoever forges a copyrighted work of fine art or imitates the artistic expression of another author with the intent that the new work be considered an original work of such author shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such act, or
c) if he commits such act on a substantial scale. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years,
a) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he commits such act on a large scale. CRIMINAL OFFENCES OF GENERAL DANGER
Criminal Offences Posing General Danger
General Danger
(1) Whoever intentionally causes general danger by exposing people to danger of death or serious injury to health or another's property to danger of large-scale damage by causing a fire or flood or harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces or by committing another similarly dangerous act, or
whoever increases such general danger or makes it more difficult to avert or mitigate it,
shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he commits such act repeatedly within a short time,
c) if he causes large-scale damage by such act, or
d) if he causes serious injury to health of at least two persons or death by such act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence,
a) if he intentionally causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. General Danger Through Negligence
(1) Whoever negligently causes general danger by exposing people to danger of death or serious injury to health or another's property to danger of large-scale damage by causing a fire or flood or harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces or by committing another similarly dangerous act, or
whoever negligently increases such general danger or makes it more difficult to avert or mitigate it,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by prohibition of activity,
a) if he causes serious injury to health by the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) if he commits such act because he breached an important obligation arising from his employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him by law, or
c) if he causes substantial damage by such act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years or by a fine,
a) if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he causes large-scale damage or serious injury to health by the act referred to in paragraph 2(b). (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 2(b). Endangerment Under the Influence of an Addictive Substance
(1) Whoever performs, in a state excluding capability, which he brought upon himself by the influence of an addictive substance, employment or other activity in which he could endanger the life or health of people or cause substantial damage to property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by a fine or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, by a fine or by prohibition of activity,
a) if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 an accident, traffic or other mishap, injury to another or greater damage to another's property or other serious consequence,
b) if he commits such act in the performance of employment or other activity in which the influence of an addictive substance is particularly dangerous, especially if he operates a mass transport vehicle, or
c) if he has been convicted of such an act in the last two years or released from imprisonment imposed for such an act. Breach of Obligation in Imminent Distress
Whoever frustrates or makes it more difficult to avert or mitigate imminent distress that directly affects a larger group of persons by, without serious reason,
a) refusing assistance that is imposed on him by law or to which he has committed himself, or
b) frustrating the provision of such assistance by another person,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. Damage to and Endangerment of the Operation of a Public Utility Facility
(1) Whoever intentionally damages a public utility facility or endangers its operation or use shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years or by a fine,
a) if he destroys, removes or renders unusable a public utility facility,
b) if he causes disruption of the operation of a public utility facility by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
c) if he causes substantial damage by such act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,
a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2(a), or
b) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Damage to and Endangerment of the Operation of a Public Utility Facility Through Negligence
(1) Whoever through gross negligence destroys, damages, removes or renders unusable a public utility facility or endangers the operation of a public utility facility shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years,
a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Damage to a Geodetic Point
(1) Whoever intentionally destroys, damages or renders unusable the mark of
a) a geodetic point of the basic geodynamic network,
b) a geodetic point of the zero-order reference network,
c) a geodetic point of the supplemented zero-order reference network,
d) a basic levelling geodetic point, or
e) an absolute gravity geodetic point,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by a fine. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever intentionally destroys, damages or renders unusable the equipment of a permanent station for receiving signals from global navigation systems of the Czech Network of Permanent Stations for Position Determination. Unlawful Arming
(1) Whoever, without a permit, manufactures, procures for himself or another or stores a firearm or its main parts or components, or ammunition in a larger quantity or a prohibited weapon accessory, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on
whoever restores a deactivated weapon to firing condition or makes constructional changes to it aimed at restoring it to firing condition, or makes constructional changes to a weapon aimed at increasing its effectiveness, or
whoever forges, alters, obliterates or removes the unique marking of a firearm that enables its identification. (3) Whoever
a) manufactures, procures for himself or another or stores an explosive in a quantity greater than small, a weapon of mass destruction or components that are necessary for the use of such weapon, or
b) accumulates, manufactures or procures for himself or another weapons or ammunition in a substantial quantity,
shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 3 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he commits such act on a larger scale, or
c) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Development, Production and Possession of Prohibited Combat Means
(1) Whoever manufactures, imports, exports, transports, stores or accumulates weapons, combat means or explosives prohibited by law or international treaty, or otherwise handles them, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years or by forfeiture of property.
