(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever designs, constructs or uses facilities intended for the development, production or storage of weapons, combat means or explosives referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) if he commits such act on a larger scale, or
c) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Unlawful Production and Possession of Radioactive Material and Highly Dangerous Substance
(1) Whoever, without a permit, even through gross negligence, manufactures, imports, exports, transports, stores or procures for another radioactive material or highly dangerous substance or items intended for its production shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years, by a fine or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he causes serious injury to health by the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) if he commits such act on a larger scale, or
c) if he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years or by forfeiture of property,
a) if he causes serious injury to health of at least two persons or death by the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act,
c) if he commits such act as a member of an organised group, or
d) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Highly Dangerous Substance
For the purposes of this Act, a highly dangerous substance means a substance classified in Schedule 1 in the Annex to the Convention on the Prohibition of the Development, Production, Stockpiling and Use of Chemical Weapons and on Their Destruction. Unlawful Production and Possession of Nuclear Material and Special Fissile Material
(1) Whoever, without a permit, even through gross negligence, manufactures, imports, exports, transports, stores or procures for another source nuclear material or items intended for its production shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years. (2) Whoever, without a permit, even through gross negligence, manufactures, imports, exports, transports, stores or procures for another special fissile material or items intended for its production shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years.Unauthorised Production and Other Handling of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances and Poisons
(1) Whoever unlawfully produces, imports, exports, transports, offers, brokers, sells or otherwise procures for another or stores for another a narcotic or psychotropic substance, a preparation containing a narcotic or psychotropic substance, a precursor or a poison, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years or by forfeiture of property if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) as a member of an organised group,
b) having been convicted or punished for such an act in the last three years,
d) on a larger scale towards a child or in a quantity greater than small towards a child under fifteen years of age. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years or by forfeiture of property if
a) he causes serious bodily harm by the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) he commits such an act with the intention of obtaining substantial benefit for himself or another,
c) he commits such an act on a large scale, or
d) he commits such an act on a larger scale towards a child under fifteen years of age. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to eighteen years or by forfeiture of property if
a) he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons or death by the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) he commits such an act with the intention of obtaining large-scale benefit for himself or another, or
c) he commits such an act in connection with an organised group operating in more than one state. Possession of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances and Poisons
(1) Whoever unlawfully possesses for his own use in a quantity greater than small a narcotic substance cannabis, cannabis resin or a psychotropic substance containing any tetrahydrocannabinol, isomer or stereochemical variant thereof (THC), shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) Whoever unlawfully possesses for his own use a narcotic or psychotropic substance other than that referred to in paragraph 1 or a poison in a quantity greater than small, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 on a larger scale. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 on a substantial scale. Unauthorised Cultivation of Plants Containing Narcotic or Psychotropic Substances
(1) Whoever unlawfully cultivates for his own use in a quantity greater than small a cannabis plant, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) Whoever unlawfully cultivates for his own use in a quantity greater than small a mushroom or other plant than that referred to in paragraph 1 containing a narcotic or psychotropic substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by a fine if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 on a larger scale. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 on a substantial scale. Production and Possession of Objects for Unauthorised Production of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances and Poisons
(1) Whoever produces, procures for himself or another or possesses a precursor or other object intended for the unauthorised production of a narcotic or psychotropic substance, a preparation containing a narcotic or psychotropic substance, or a poison, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years, by a fine, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) he commits such an act on a substantial scale,
c) he commits such an act on a larger scale towards a child, or
d) he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such an act. Spreading Addiction
(1) Whoever induces another to abuse an addictive substance other than alcohol or supports him in doing so, or whoever otherwise incites or spreads the abuse of such a substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) as a member of an organised group,
c) by press, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 towards a child under fifteen years of age. Production and Other Handling of Substances with Hormonal Effects
(1) Whoever unlawfully produces, possesses, imports, exports, transports, offers, sells, provides to or administers to another in a larger quantity a substance with anabolic or other hormonal effects for purposes other than therapeutic, or whoever uses towards another a method consisting in increasing the transfer of oxygen in the human organism or another method with a doping effect for purposes other than therapeutic, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to three years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) he commits such an act towards a child under fifteen years of age, or
c) he commits such an act on a substantial scale towards a child over fifteen years of age. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he causes serious bodily harm by the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) he commits such an act with the intention of obtaining substantial benefit for himself or another,
c) he commits such an act on a substantial scale towards a child under fifteen years of age,
d) he commits such an act as a physician or other qualified health professional. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if
a) he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons or death by the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) he commits such an act with the intention of obtaining large-scale benefit for himself or another, or
c) he commits such an act in connection with an organised group operating in more than one state. Common Provision
(1) The law stipulates what is considered to be narcotic substances, psychotropic substances, preparations containing narcotic or psychotropic substances, precursors used for the illicit production of narcotic or psychotropic substances. (2) The Government shall stipulate by decree what is considered to be poisons within the meaning of §§ 283, 284 and 286. (3) The Government shall stipulate by decree which plants or mushrooms are considered to be plants and mushrooms containing narcotic or psychotropic substances pursuant to § 285. (4) The Government shall stipulate by decree what is considered to be substances with anabolic and other hormonal effects and what is a larger quantity within the meaning of § 288, and what is considered to be a method consisting in increasing the transfer of oxygen in the human organism and other methods with a doping effect within the meaning of § 288. Criminal Offences Endangering Aircraft, Civil Vessels and Fixed Platforms
Seizure of Control over Aircraft, Civil Vessels and Fixed Platforms
(1) Whoever on board an aircraft, civil vessel or on a fixed platform on the continental shelf, with the intention of seizing or exercising control over such aircraft, civil vessel or fixed platform,
a) uses violence or threats of imminent violence against another,
b) threatens another with killing, causing bodily harm or causing large-scale damage, or
c) takes advantage of another's defencelessness,
shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years or by forfeiture of property. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence, possibly in addition to these sentences also by forfeiture of property, if
a) he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons or death by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Endangering the Safety of Aircraft and Civil Vessels
Whoever communicates false information that may endanger the safety or operation of an aircraft in flight or a civil vessel at sea, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. Hijacking of Aircraft to a Foreign Country
(1) Whoever, for the purpose of hijacking an aircraft to a foreign country, seizes such an aircraft or unlawfully uses such an aircraft that has been entrusted to him, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence, if he causes serious bodily harm by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence, possibly in addition to these sentences also by forfeiture of property, if
a) he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons or death by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST THE ENVIRONMENT
Damage to and Endangering of the Environment
(1) Whoever, in breach of another legal regulation, intentionally damages or endangers soil, water, air or another component of the environment, on a larger scale or over a larger territory, or in such a manner that it may cause serious bodily harm or death or if the removal of the consequences of such conduct requires the expenditure of costs on a substantial scale, or
whoever intentionally increases such damage to or endangering of a component of the environment or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 repeatedly,
b) he commits such an act because he violated an important duty arising from his employment, occupation, position or function or imposed on him by law,
c) he causes permanent or long-term damage to a component of the environment by such an act,
d) the removal of the consequences of such an act requires the expenditure of costs on a large scale, or
e) he commits such an act with the intention of obtaining substantial benefit for himself or another. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits such an act with the intention of obtaining large-scale benefit for himself or another. Damage to and Endangering of the Environment by Negligence
(1) Whoever, in breach of another legal regulation, by gross negligence damages or endangers soil, water, air or another component of the environment, on a larger scale or over a larger territory, or in such a manner that it may cause serious bodily harm or death or if the removal of the consequences of such conduct requires the expenditure of costs on a substantial scale, or
whoever by gross negligence increases such damage to or endangering of a component of the environment or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 because he violated an important duty arising from his employment, occupation, position or function or imposed on him by law,
b) he causes permanent or long-term damage to a component of the environment by such an act, or
c) the removal of the consequences of such an act requires the expenditure of costs on a large scale. Damage to a Water Source
Whoever, even by gross negligence, causes damage to a water source for which a protection zone has been established, such that the reason for special protection of the water source ceases to exist or is substantially weakened, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. Damage to Forests
(1) Whoever, even by negligence, by harvesting forest stands or by other activity carried out in breach of another legal regulation causes, even by joining to an already existing clearing, the creation of a clear-cut area or causes serious damage to a forest over a total larger forest area or thins a forest stand below the stocking limit established by another legal regulation over a total larger forest area, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years or by prohibition of activity if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 repeatedly, or
b) the harvesting or other activity referred to in paragraph 1 creates a clear-cut area or thinning over a total substantial forest area. Common Provision
(1) A larger territory pursuant to §§ 293 and 294 means a territory with an area of at least three hectares and in the case of surface waters in water bodies, with the exception of watercourses, at least one hectare of water surface and in the case of a watercourse at least two kilometres of its length; individual affected territories and lengths of watercourses are added together. (2) A total larger forest area pursuant to § 295 means an area greater than one and a half hectares and a total substantial forest area means an area greater than three hectares. (3) Damage to or endangering of soil, water, air or another component of the environment also includes operating a facility in which a hazardous activity is carried out or in which hazardous substances or mixtures are stored or used, without a permit pursuant to another legal regulation. Unauthorised Discharge of Polluting Substances
(1) Whoever, in breach of an international treaty, even by negligence, discharges or, although obliged to do so, fails to prevent the discharge of oil, toxic liquid or other similar polluting substance from a ship or other seagoing vessel, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) as a member of an organised group, or
(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1
b) serious and extensive damage to the quality of water, animal or plant species or parts thereof, or
c) damage to the environment, the removal of which requires the expenditure of costs on a large scale. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Unauthorised Handling of Waste
(1) Whoever, even by negligence, violates another legal regulation governing waste management by transporting waste across state borders without notification or consent of the competent public authority, or provides false or grossly distorted information in such notification or application for consent or in attached documents or conceals material information, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever, even by negligence, in breach of another legal regulation deposits or discards waste, transports it or otherwise handles it, and thereby causes damage to or endangering of the environment, the removal of which requires the expenditure of costs on a substantial scale, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activity if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such an act, or
c) he commits such an act repeatedly. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if
a) he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) such an act concerns hazardous waste. Unauthorised Production and Other Handling of Ozone-Depleting Substances
(1) Whoever, in breach of another legal regulation, even by gross negligence, produces, imports, exports, places on the market an ozone-depleting substance or otherwise handles it, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 repeatedly,
b) he commits such an act with the intention of obtaining substantial benefit for himself or another, or
c) he commits such an act on a substantial scale. Unauthorised Handling of Protected Wild Animals and Wild Plants
(1) Whoever, in breach of another legal regulation, kills, destroys, damages, removes from nature, processes, imports, exports, transports, possesses, offers, brokers, procures for himself or another an individual of a specially protected species of animal or plant or a specimen of a protected species and commits such an act on more than twenty-five pieces of animals, plants or specimens, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, in breach of another legal regulation, kills, destroys, damages, removes from nature, processes, imports, exports, transports, possesses, offers, brokers, procures for himself or another an individual of a severely or critically endangered species of animal or plant or a specimen of a species directly threatened with extinction. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) he commits such an act with the intention of obtaining substantial benefit for himself or another, or
c) he causes long-term or irreversible damage to the population of a wild animal or wild plant or to the local population or habitat of a specially protected species of animal or plant by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in connection with an organised group operating in more than one state,
b) he commits such an act with the intention of obtaining large-scale benefit for himself or another, or
c) he causes long-term or irreversible damage to the local population or habitat of a critically endangered species of animal or plant by such an act. Unauthorised Handling of Protected Wild Animals and Wild Plants by Negligence
Whoever by gross negligence violates another legal regulation by killing, destroying, damaging, removing from nature, processing, repeatedly importing, exporting or transporting, possessing, offering, brokering or procuring for himself or another an individual of a specially protected species of animal or plant or a specimen of a protected species on a larger scale than twenty-five pieces or an individual of a critically endangered species of animal or plant or a specimen of a species directly threatened with extinction, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. Damage to Protected Parts of Nature
Whoever, even by gross negligence, violates another legal regulation by damaging or destroying a memorial tree, significant landscape element, cave, specially protected area, site of European importance or bird area such that the reason for protection of such part of nature ceases to exist or is substantially weakened, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. Cruelty to Animals
(1) Whoever tortures an animal in a cruel or tormenting manner, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by prohibition of activity if
a) he commits such an act publicly or in a place accessible to the public,
b) he commits such an act as a member of an organised group, or
c) he continues to commit such an act for a longer period. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a larger number of animals,
b) he causes permanent health consequences or death to the tortured animal by such an act,
c) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a particularly cruel or tormenting manner, or
d) he commits such an act repeatedly. Keeping Animals in Unsuitable Conditions
(1) Whoever keeps a larger number of animals in unsuitable conditions and thereby endangers their lives or causes them considerable suffering, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever keeps animals in unsuitable conditions for the purpose of trade, or whoever profits from such keeping, and thereby endangers their lives or causes them considerable suffering, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years or by prohibition of activity. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 2 with the intention of obtaining substantial benefit for himself or another,
b) he causes permanent consequences or death to an animal by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
c) he commits such an act as a member of an organised group. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 2 with the intention of obtaining large-scale benefit for himself or another,
b) he causes permanent consequences or death to a larger number of animals by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
c) he commits such an act in connection with an organised group operating in more than one state. Neglect of Care for an Animal by Negligence
(1) Whoever by gross negligence neglects necessary care for an animal that he owns or for which he is obliged to care for another reason, and thereby causes it permanent health consequences or death, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he causes death or permanent health consequences to a larger number of animals by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Poaching
(1) Whoever unlawfully hunts game or fish of not insignificant value or conceals, transfers to himself or another or possesses unlawfully hunted game or fish of not insignificant value, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) he obtains greater benefit for himself or another by such an act,
c) he commits such an act as a person who has a specially imposed duty to protect the environment,
d) he commits such an act in a particularly reprehensible manner, by a mass-effective method or during a closed season, or
e) he was convicted or punished for such an act in the last three years. Unauthorised Production, Possession and Other Handling of Medicinal Products and Other Substances Affecting the Productivity of Farm Animals
(1) Whoever unlawfully produces, imports, exports, transports, offers, brokers, sells or otherwise procures or possesses a substance with thyreostatic, gestagenic, androgenic, oestrogenic or other hormonal effects, beta-agonists or another substance intended to stimulate the productivity of farm animals or a preparation containing such a substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, in breach of another legal regulation, uses medicinal products for the purpose of increasing the productivity of farm animals or produces, imports, exports, transports, offers, brokers, sells or otherwise procures or possesses them for that purpose. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2
a) as a member of an organised group,
c) with the intention of obtaining substantial benefit for himself or another, or
(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2
a) as a member of an organised group operating in more than one state,
b) with the intention of obtaining large-scale benefit for himself or another, or
Spreading Contagious Animal Diseases
(1) Whoever, even by negligence, causes or increases the danger of introduction or spread of a contagious disease of animals in hobby farms, farm animals or wild animals, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he causes the spread of such a disease by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Spreading Contagious Diseases and Pests of Useful Plants
(1) Whoever, even by negligence, causes or increases the danger of introduction or spread of a contagious disease or pest of useful plants, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he causes the spread of such a disease or such a pest by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Common Provision
The Government shall stipulate by decree which diseases of animals and plants are considered contagious within the meaning of §§ 306 and 307 and to which pests the provision of § 307 applies. CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST THE CZECH REPUBLIC, A FOREIGN STATE AND AN INTERNATIONAL ORGANISATION
Criminal Offences against the Foundations of the Czech Republic, a Foreign State and an International Organisation
High Treason
(1) A citizen of the Czech Republic who, in connection with a foreign power or a foreign agent, commits the criminal offence of subversion of the republic (§ 310), terrorist attack (§ 311), terror (§ 312) or sabotage (§ 314), shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence. Subversion of the Republic
(1) Whoever, with the intention of subverting the constitutional system, territorial integrity or defence capability of the Czech Republic or destroying its independence, participates in violent actions against the Czech Republic or its organs, shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence, if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons or death by such an act,
c) he causes large-scale damage by such an act,
d) he causes serious endangering of the international position of the Czech Republic by such an act, or
e) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Terrorist Attack
(1) Whoever, with the intention of damaging the constitutional system or defence capability of the Czech Republic, disrupting or destroying the basic political, economic or social structure of the Czech Republic or an international organisation, seriously intimidating the population or unlawfully compelling the government or another public authority or international organisation to do, omit or tolerate something,
a) destroys or damages to a greater extent public space, property or public facilities, a transport or telecommunications system, a fixed platform on the continental shelf, energy, water supply, health or other important facilities, including a computer system on whose functioning such facility, system or platform depends, with the aim of putting property at risk of large-scale damage,
b) disrupts or interrupts the supply of water, electricity or another basic natural resource with the aim of putting property at risk of large-scale damage,
c) seizes an aircraft, ship, other means of personal or freight transport or a fixed platform on the continental shelf or exercises control over such means of transport or fixed platform or destroys or seriously damages navigation equipment or interferes to a greater extent with its operation or communicates important false information, thereby putting property at risk of large-scale damage,
d) puts another's property at risk of large-scale damage by causing a fire or flood or harmful effects of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces or commits other similar dangerous conduct, or increases such danger or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, or
e) by inserting or transferring data into a computer system or onto an information carrier, making another intervention in the software or technical equipment of a computer system or other technical device for data processing or by erasing or otherwise destroying, damaging, altering or suppressing data stored in a computer system or on an information carrier, reducing their quality or rendering them unusable, carries out an attack against a computer system whose disruption would have a serious impact on the functioning of the state, the health of persons, security, the economy or ensuring the basic living needs of the population, an attack with an impact on a larger number of computer systems using a computer program created or adapted for such an attack or an attack by which he causes substantial damage,
shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property. (2) Whoever, with the intention of damaging the constitutional system or defence capability of the Czech Republic, disrupting or destroying the basic political, economic or social structure of the Czech Republic or an international organisation, seriously intimidating the population or unlawfully compelling the government or another public authority or international organisation to do, omit or tolerate something,
a) carries out an attack endangering the life or health of a person with the aim of causing death or serious bodily harm,
b) seizes a hostage or carries out an abduction,
c) destroys or damages to a greater extent public space, property or public facilities, a transport or telecommunications system, a fixed platform on the continental shelf, energy, water supply, health or other important facilities, including a computer system on whose functioning such facility, system or platform depends, with the aim of thereby endangering human lives or the safety of such space, facility, system or platform,
d) disrupts or interrupts the supply of water, electricity or another basic natural resource with the aim of thereby endangering human lives,
e) seizes an aircraft, ship, other means of personal or freight transport or a fixed platform on the continental shelf or exercises control over such means of transport or fixed platform or destroys or seriously damages navigation equipment or interferes to a greater extent with its operation or communicates important false information, thereby endangering the life or health of people or the safety of such means of transport,
f) produces or otherwise obtains, possesses, imports, transports, exports or otherwise supplies or uses an explosive, nuclear material, nuclear, biological, chemical or other weapon, combat means or material of a similar nature, or conducts research and development of a nuclear, biological, chemical or other weapon or combat means or explosive, or
g) puts people in general danger of death or serious bodily harm by causing a fire or flood or harmful effects of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces or commits other similar dangerous conduct, or increases such general danger or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult,
shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence, if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) he causes serious bodily harm or death by such an act,
c) he causes a larger number of people to be left without shelter by such an act,
d) he causes large-scale damage by such an act,
e) he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such an act,
f) he seriously endangers the international position of the Czech Republic or the position of an international organisation of which the Czech Republic is a member by such an act, or
g) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Terror
(1) Whoever, with the intention of damaging the constitutional system of the Czech Republic, intentionally kills another, shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence. Participation in a Terrorist Group
(1) Whoever establishes a terrorist group or whoever participates in the activity of a terrorist group, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) as a leading functionary or representative of a terrorist group, or
b) as a founder or member of a terrorist group that meets the characteristics of an organised criminal group.(3) By imprisonment for twelve to twenty years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) as a leading functionary or representative of a terrorist group that meets the characteristics of an organized criminal group, or
b) during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. (4) The provisions of Sections 107 and 108 shall not apply to the offender of the act referred to in paragraphs 1 to 3. Special provision on effective remorse
Whoever commits the act referred to in Section 312a shall not be punishable if he made a report about the terrorist group to a public prosecutor or police authority at a time when the danger that arose to an interest protected by this Act from another act of the terrorist group, other than that referred to in Section 312a, could still be averted. A soldier may also make such a report to a superior. Impunity of an agent
(1) A police officer performing tasks as an agent pursuant to another legal regulation (hereinafter "agent") who participates in the activities of a terrorist group shall not be punishable for the criminal offence of participation in a terrorist group pursuant to Section 312a if he committed such an act with the aim of detecting the perpetrator of criminal activity committed by members of the terrorist group, in connection with the terrorist group or for the benefit of the terrorist group, or to prevent its commission. (2) An agent who participates in the activities of a terrorist group or supports such a group shall not be punishable for the criminal offence of affray pursuant to Section 158(1), trafficking in human beings (Section 168), entrusting a child to the power of another (Section 169), violation of domiciliary freedom (Section 178), damage to the rights of another (Section 181), violation of the secrecy of transmitted messages (Section 182), procuring (Section 189), dissemination of pornography (Section 191), production and other handling of child pornography (Section 192), abuse of a child for the production of pornography (Section 193), theft (Section 205), embezzlement (Section 206), unauthorized use of another's property (Section 207), operating dishonest games and betting (Section 213), legalization of proceeds of crime (Section 216), machinations in insolvency proceedings (Section 226), breach of the obligation to make a truthful declaration of assets (Section 227), damage to another's property (Section 228), counterfeiting and alteration of money (Section 233), unauthorized procurement, counterfeiting and alteration of a payment instrument (Section 234), uttering counterfeit and altered money (Section 235), production and possession of counterfeiting tools and equipment intended for unauthorized acquisition of a payment instrument (Section 236), unauthorized production of money (Section 237), breach of regulations on stamps and other items for marking goods (Section 244), counterfeiting and alteration of items for marking goods for tax purposes and items proving fulfilment of a fee obligation (Section 245), breach of prohibitions during a state of emergency in foreign exchange management (Section 247), unauthorized business activity (Section 251), unauthorized operation of gambling (Section 252), procuring an advantage in the award of a public contract, in a public competition and public auction (Section 256), machinations in the award of a public contract and in a public competition (Section 257), machinations in a public auction (Section 258), breach of regulations on the circulation of goods in dealings with foreign countries (Section 261), breach of regulations on export control of dual-use goods and technologies (Section 262), breach of obligations in the export of dual-use goods and technologies (Section 263), distortion of data and failure to keep records regarding the export of dual-use goods and technologies (Section 264), conducting foreign trade in military material without a permit or licence (Section 265), breach of obligations in connection with the issue of a permit and licence for foreign trade in military material (Section 266), distortion of data and failure to keep records regarding foreign trade in military material (Section 267), damage to and endangering the operation of a generally beneficial facility (Section 276), unlawful arming (Section 279), development, production and possession of prohibited combat means (Section 280), unlawful production and possession of radioactive material and highly hazardous material (Section 281), unlawful production and possession of nuclear material and special fissile material (Section 282), unlawful production and other handling of narcotic and psychotropic substances and poisons (Section 283), possession of a narcotic and psychotropic substance and poison (Section 284), unlawful cultivation of plants containing a narcotic or psychotropic substance (Section 285), production and possession of an item for the unlawful production of a narcotic and psychotropic substance and poison (Section 286), production and other handling of substances with hormonal effects (Section 288), unauthorized handling of waste (Section 298), unauthorized handling of protected wild animals and wild plants (Section 299), financing of terrorism (Section 312d), support and promotion of terrorism (Section 312e), threatening with a terrorist criminal offence (Section 312f), abuse of authority by an official (Section 329), acceptance of a bribe (Section 331), bribery (Section 332), indirect bribery pursuant to Section 333(2), organizing and enabling unlawful crossing of the state border (Section 340), aiding unauthorized stay on the territory of the Republic (Section 341), counterfeiting and alteration of a public document (Section 348), dangerous threatening (Section 353), defamation of a nation, race, ethnic or other group (Section 355), incitement to hatred against a group of persons or to restriction of their rights and freedoms (Section 356) or disorderly conduct (Section 358), if he committed such an act with the aim of detecting the perpetrator of criminal activity committed by members of the terrorist group, in connection with the terrorist group or for the benefit of the terrorist group, or to prevent its commission. (3) An agent who founded or organized the terrorist group, or an agent who is a leading functionary or representative of the terrorist group, shall not become unpunishable. Financing of terrorism
(1) Whoever, himself or through another, financially or materially supports a terrorist group, its member, a terrorist or the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, the criminal offence of support and promotion of terrorism (Section 312e) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offence (Section 312f), or collects financial resources or other things with the intention that they be used in such a manner, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property. (2) By imprisonment for five to fifteen years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) as a member of an organized group,
b) during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, or
Support and promotion of terrorism
(1) Whoever publicly incites the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, or
whoever publicly approves a committed terrorist criminal offence or publicly praises its perpetrator for it,
shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years. (2) By imprisonment for three to twelve years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property, shall be punished,
a) whoever recruits another to commit a terrorist criminal offence or the criminal offence of participation in a terrorist group (Section 312a),
b) whoever provides another with information or training concerning the production or use of explosives, weapons, hazardous substances or materials of a similar nature or other similar methods or techniques for the purposes of committing a terrorist criminal offence,
c) whoever obtains information or acquires skills concerning the production or use of explosives, weapons, hazardous substances or materials of a similar nature or other similar methods or techniques for the purpose of committing a terrorist criminal offence, or
d) whoever, himself or through another, provides or promises a reward or compensation to another or for another for the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, or organizes a collection for such a reward or compensation. (3) In the same manner as in paragraph 2 shall be punished,
a) whoever travels to another state or to the Czech Republic for the purpose of committing a terrorist criminal offence, the criminal offence of participation in a terrorist group (Section 312a), threatening with a terrorist criminal offence (Section 312f) or the criminal offence referred to in paragraph 2(b) or (c), or
b) whoever organizes such travel for another, induces another to it, or enables or facilitates another to travel in such a manner. (4) By imprisonment for five to fifteen years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2(b) or (d) through the press, film, radio, television, a publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means,
b) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 as a member of an organized group,
c) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, or
d) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 2(a) or (b) against a child. Threatening with a terrorist criminal offence
(1) Whoever threatens the commission of a terrorist criminal offence shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property. (2) By imprisonment for five to fifteen years, or in addition to this penalty by forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) as a member of an organized group,
b) through the press, film, radio, television, a publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means,
d) during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Common provision
Protection pursuant to Sections 311 and 312 shall also be provided to a foreign state. Sabotage
(1) Whoever, with the intention of damaging the constitutional system or defence capability of the Czech Republic or damaging an international organization, abuses his employment, profession, position or function or commits another act in order to
a) frustrate or hinder the fulfilment of an important task of an international organization, a public authority, the armed forces or a security corps, an economic organization or other institution, or
b) cause a disruption or other serious damage in the activities of such an authority or such an organization or institution,
shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property. (2) By imprisonment for eight to twelve years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group, or
b) if he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. (3) By imprisonment for ten to twenty years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence, the offender shall be punished,
a) if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons or death,
b) if by such an act he causes damage on a large scale,
c) if by such an act he seriously endangers the international standing of the Czech Republic or the standing of an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member, or
d) if he organizes such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Abuse of representation of the state and international organization
(1) Whoever, with the intention of damaging the constitutional system, territorial integrity or defence capability of the Czech Republic or destroying its independence or damaging an international organization, although entrusted with representing the Czech Republic or an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member, or negotiating with a foreign power or otherwise protecting the interests of the Czech Republic or an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member, in dealings with a person representing the interests of a foreign power, abuses his authority to act for the Czech Republic or the said international organization, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years. (2) In the same manner shall be punished whoever, with such an intention, pretends to be an entrusted person and enters into negotiations on important matters of the Czech Republic or an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member with a person representing the interests of a foreign power. (3) By imprisonment for eight to twelve years the offender shall be punished,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,
b) if by such an act he causes serious endangerment of the international standing of the Czech Republic or the standing of an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member, or
c) if he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Criminal offences against the security of the Czech Republic, a foreign state and an international organization
Espionage
(1) Whoever spies on information classified pursuant to another legal regulation, the misuse of which may seriously endanger or damage the constitutional system, sovereignty, territorial integrity, defence and security of the Czech Republic or another state or the defence and security of an international organization, for the protection of whose interests in the said areas the Czech Republic has undertaken an obligation, with the aim of disclosing it to a foreign power,
whoever, with such an aim, collects data containing classified information, or
whoever intentionally discloses such classified information to a foreign power,
shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years. (2) In the same manner shall be punished whoever enables or facilitates the activity of the perpetrator of the act referred to in paragraph 1 or an organization whose aim is to spy on classified information. (3) By imprisonment for eight to fifteen years the offender shall be punished,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organization whose aim is to spy on classified information,
b) if he commits such an act although the protection of classified information was specially imposed on him,
c) if by such an act he procures for himself or another a substantial benefit or commits such an act on a substantial scale, or
d) if such an act concerns classified information classified in another legal regulation with the classification level "Top Secret". (4) By imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Endangering classified information
(1) Whoever spies on information classified pursuant to another legal regulation with the aim of disclosing it to an unauthorized person, whoever, with such an aim, collects data containing classified information, or whoever intentionally discloses such classified information to an unauthorized person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) By imprisonment for two to eight years the offender shall be punished,
a) if he intentionally discloses to an unauthorized person classified information classified in another legal regulation with the classification level "Top Secret" or "Secret",
b) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 although the protection of classified information was specially imposed on him, or
c) if by such an act he procures for himself or another a substantial benefit or causes substantial damage or another particularly serious consequence. (3) By imprisonment for five to twelve years the offender shall be punished,
a) if the act referred to in paragraph 1 concerns classified information from the area of ensuring the defence capability of the Czech Republic classified in another legal regulation with the classification level "Top Secret", or
b) if he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Endangering classified information by negligence
Whoever by negligence causes the disclosure of classified information classified in another legal regulation with the classification level "Top Secret" or "Secret" shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. Criminal offences against the defence of the state
Cooperation with the enemy
(1) Whoever, during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, procures a benefit for the enemy or supports him in any way shall be punished, unless it is an act subject to stricter punishment, by imprisonment for one to ten years. War treason
(1) A citizen of the Czech Republic who, during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, serves in enemy armed forces shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence. Service in foreign armed forces
(1) A citizen of the Czech Republic who, in breach of another legal regulation, serves in the army or armed forces of another state shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years. (2) By imprisonment for three to ten years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Participation in a non-state armed group aimed at operating in an armed conflict
Whoever participates in the activities of a non-state armed group aimed at operating in an armed conflict taking place on the territory of another state by
a) engaging in combat activities of such a group,
b) providing another with information or training concerning the production or use of explosives, weapons, hazardous substances or materials of a similar nature or other similar methods or techniques for the purpose of committing the act referred to in letter (a),
c) obtaining information or acquiring skills concerning the production or use of explosives, weapons, hazardous substances or materials of a similar nature or other similar methods or techniques for the purpose of committing the act referred to in letter (a) or (b), or
d) travelling to another state or to the Czech Republic for the purpose of committing the act referred to in letter (a), (b) or (c),
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years. Breach of personal and material obligation for the defence of the state
(1) Whoever fails to fulfil, even by negligence, a personal or material obligation for the defence of the state,
whoever intentionally evades the fulfilment of such an obligation, or
whoever frustrates or hinders the fulfilment of such an obligation by another person, even by negligence,
shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. (2) By imprisonment for two to eight years the offender shall be punished if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he endangers to a substantial extent the interests of the defence of the state. CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST ORDER IN PUBLIC AFFAIRS
Criminal offences against the exercise of authority of a public authority and an official
Violence against a public authority
(1) Whoever uses violence with the intention of influencing the exercise of authority of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. (2) By imprisonment for two to eight years the offender shall be punished,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon,
b) if by such an act he causes bodily harm to another, or
c) if by such an act he causes substantial damage. (3) By imprisonment for four to twelve years the offender shall be punished if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes
b) damage on a large scale, or
c) a disruption in the activities of such an authority. (4) By imprisonment for ten to eighteen years the offender shall be punished if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes death. Threatening with the aim of influencing a public authority
(1) Whoever threatens another with killing, causing bodily harm or causing substantial damage
a) with the intention of influencing the exercise of authority of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority, or
b) for the exercise of authority of such an authority,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) By imprisonment for up to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon. Violence against an official
a) with the intention of influencing the exercise of authority of an official, or
b) for the exercise of authority of an official,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years. (2) By imprisonment for six months to six years the offender shall be punished,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon,
b) if by such an act he causes bodily harm to another, or
c) if by such an act he causes greater damage. (3) By imprisonment for three to twelve years the offender shall be punished if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes
a) serious bodily harm, or
(4) By imprisonment for eight to sixteen years the offender shall be punished if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes death. Threatening with the aim of influencing an official
(1) Whoever threatens another with killing, causing bodily harm or causing substantial damage
a) with the intention of influencing the exercise of authority of an official, or
b) for the exercise of authority of an official,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) By imprisonment for up to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon. Common provision
(1) Protection pursuant to Sections 323 to 326 shall also be provided to a person who came forward in support or protection of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority or an official. (2) Protection pursuant to Sections 323 to 326 shall also be provided to an internationally protected person, which means an official of a foreign state or international organization who enjoys diplomatic or other privileges and immunities under international law, or a person holding a position or employed or working in an international judicial body. Usurpation of authority of an office
Whoever unlawfully performs acts that are reserved to a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority, or
whoever performs an act that may be performed only by virtue of official authority of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. Criminal offences of officials
Abuse of authority by an official
(1) An official who, with the intention of causing damage or other serious harm to another or procuring an unauthorized benefit for himself or another
a) exercises his authority in a manner contrary to another legal regulation,
b) exceeds his authority, or
c) fails to fulfil an obligation arising from his authority,
shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by prohibition of activity. (2) By imprisonment for three to ten years the offender shall be punished,
a) if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he procures for himself or another a substantial benefit,
b) if he commits such an act against another for his actual or supposed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion,
c) if by such an act he causes a serious disruption in the activities of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority,
d) if by such an act he causes a serious disruption in the activities of a legal person or natural person who is an entrepreneur,
e) if he commits such an act by exploiting the defencelessness, dependence, distress, mental weakness or inexperience of another, or
f) if by such an act he causes substantial damage. (3) By imprisonment for five to twelve years or by forfeiture of property the offender shall be punished,
a) if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he procures for himself or another a benefit on a large scale, or
b) if by such an act he causes damage on a large scale. Frustration of the task of an official by negligence
(1) An official who, in the exercise of his authority, by negligence frustrates or substantially hinders the fulfilment of an important task shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) By imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity the offender shall be punished,
a) if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes a serious disruption in the activities of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority,
b) if by such an act he causes a serious disruption in the activities of a legal person or natural person who is an entrepreneur,
c) if by such an act he causes substantial damage, or
d) if by such an act he secures for another a substantial benefit. (3) By imprisonment for one to five years the offender shall be punished,
a) if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes damage on a large scale, or
b) if by such an act he secures for another a benefit on a large scale. Bribery
Acceptance of a bribe
(1) Whoever, himself or through another, in connection with handling matters of general interest, accepts or has promised for himself or another a bribe, or
whoever, himself or through another, in connection with his or another's business, accepts or has promised for himself or another a bribe,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years or by prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever, under the circumstances referred to in paragraph 1, requests a bribe shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. (3) By imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property the offender shall be punished,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 with the intention of procuring for himself or another a substantial benefit, or
b) if he commits such an act as an official. (4) By imprisonment for five to twelve years the offender shall be punished,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 with the intention of procuring for himself or another a benefit on a large scale, or
b) if he commits such an act as an official with the intention of procuring for himself or another a substantial benefit. Bribery
(1) Whoever, himself or through another, provides, offers or promises a bribe to another or for another in connection with handling matters of general interest, or
whoever, himself or through another, provides, offers or promises a bribe to another or for another in connection with his or another's business,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by a fine. (2) By imprisonment for one to six years, forfeiture of property or a fine the offender shall be punished,
a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with the intention of procuring for himself or another a substantial benefit or causing another substantial damage or another particularly serious consequence, or
b) if he commits such an act towards an official. Indirect bribery
(1) Whoever, himself or through another, requests, has promised or accepts a bribe for using his influence or through another to influence the exercise of authority of an official, or for having already done so, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) Whoever, himself or through another, for the reason referred to in paragraph 1, provides, offers or promises a bribe to another shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. Common provision
(1) A bribe means an unauthorized advantage consisting in direct material enrichment or other preferential treatment that is or is to be given to the bribed person or, with his consent, to another person, and to which there is no entitlement. (2) An official pursuant to Sections 331 to 333 means, in addition to the person referred to in Section 127, also any person
a) holding a position in a legislative body, judicial body or other public authority of a foreign state,
b) holding a position or employed or working in an international judicial body,
c) holding a position or employed or working in an international or supranational organization created by states or other subjects of public international law or in its body or institution, or acting on behalf of such an organization, or
d) holding a position in a business legal person in which the Czech Republic or a foreign state has a decisive influence,
if the exercise of such a position, employment or work is associated with authority in handling matters of general interest and the criminal offence was committed in connection with this authority. (3) Handling matters of general interest shall also be deemed to include observing an obligation imposed by a legal regulation or contractually assumed, the purpose of which is to ensure that in business relations there is no damage to or unjustified preferential treatment of the participants in these relations or persons acting on their behalf. Other disruptions of the activities of a public authority
Interference with the independence of a court
(1) Whoever influences a judge to breach his obligations in proceedings before a court shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. (2) By imprisonment for two to ten years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with the intention of
a) procuring for himself or another a substantial benefit,
b) causing substantial damage, or
c) seriously harming another in employment, disrupting his family relations or causing him other serious harm. (3) By imprisonment for five to twelve years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with the intention of
a) procuring for himself or another a benefit on a large scale, or
b) causing damage on a large scale. Contempt of court
a) seriously disrupts court proceedings,
b) behaves insultingly towards the court or disparages the court during such proceedings, or
c) without sufficient excuse disobeys an order or summons of the court or frustrates court proceedings,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by forfeiture of a thing. Frustration of the execution of an official decision and expulsion
(1) Whoever frustrates or substantially hinders the execution of a decision of a court or other public authority by
a) carrying out an activity that was prohibited to him by such a decision or for which the relevant authorization was withdrawn from him pursuant to another legal regulation or for which he lost such authorization,
b) entering the territory of the Czech Republic although he was imposed a penalty of expulsion or a decision was made on his administrative expulsion, or staying on its territory despite the imposed penalty of expulsion or decision on administrative expulsion after measures aimed at his departure were used against him without result,
c) without permission and without serious reason staying in a place or district to which the penalty of prohibition of residence applies, or not observing the restrictions that were imposed on him by the court in connection with the execution of this penalty,
d) repeatedly or systematically engaging in conduct to frustrate the purpose of a restrictive measure imposed in proceedings on a misdemeanour, which consists in a prohibition on visiting places where sporting events are held, or to frustrate the purpose of a restrictive measure imposed in proceedings on a misdemeanour pursuant to the Act on Addictive Substances, which consists in a prohibition on visiting certain publicly accessible places,
e) attending an event to which the penalty of prohibition of entry to sporting, cultural and other social events applies, or engaging in other serious conduct to frustrate the purpose of this penalty,
f) keeping or breeding an animal to which the penalty of prohibition of keeping and breeding animals applies, or engaging in other serious conduct to frustrate the purpose of this penalty,
g) without serious reason failing to commence serving a penalty of imprisonment upon summons by the court or otherwise unlawfully resisting commencement of the execution of this penalty,
h) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the execution or purpose of a penalty,
i) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the execution or purpose of custody,
j) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the execution or purpose of security detention, or
k) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the execution or purpose of protective treatment or protective education that were imposed by a court, or otherwise, in particular by escape from an institution, assistance in escape, substantially hindering the execution of such decisions, or frustrating supervision imposed upon termination of protective treatment,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) In the same manner shall be punished whoever engages in serious or repeated conduct to frustrate an expulsion carried out pursuant to another legal regulation or a decision on a preliminary measure of a court imposing an obligation to temporarily leave a common dwelling and its immediate surroundings and refrain from entering it, or an obligation to refrain from contact with the applicant and establishing contacts with him. (3) Whoever frustrates or substantially hinders the execution of a decision of a court or other public authority by
a) destroying, damaging, rendering unusable, concealing, alienating or removing a thing to which such a decision relates, or
b) escaping from a guard, from custody, from the execution of a penalty of imprisonment or security detention,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years or by a fine. (4) Whoever, after measures in civil court proceedings aimed at the execution of a decision of a court or a court-approved agreement on the upbringing of minor children, including arrangements for contact with a child, were used against him without result, frustrates the execution of such a decision or agreement, or
whoever engages in serious conduct to frustrate the execution of a decision of another public authority concerning the upbringing of minor children,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. Liberation of a prisoner
(1) Whoever liberates a prisoner or inmate in the execution of security detention, organizes his escape, induces him to escape or prepares his escape shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (2) By imprisonment for one to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
Violent crossing of the state border
(1) Whoever crosses the state border using violence or threat of imminent violence shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. (2) By imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property the offender shall be punished if
a) he organizes the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) he commits such an act with a weapon or with at least two persons,
c) he commits such an act with the intention of concealing or facilitating another criminal offence,
d) he causes serious bodily harm by such an act,
e) he causes substantial damage by such an act, or
f) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. (3) By imprisonment for eight to fifteen years, or in addition to this penalty also by forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished,
a) if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes death,
b) if by such an act he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons,
c) if by such an act he causes damage on a large scale, or
d) if he commits such an act as a soldier during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Organizing and enabling unlawful crossing of the state border
(1) Whoever organizes for another an unlawful crossing of the state border or enables or assists another to unlawfully cross the state border, or enables or assists another after unlawful crossing of the state border to be transported across the territory of the Czech Republic, or organizes such transport, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity.(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, forfeiture of property or a fine if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) he subjects another to inhuman or degrading treatment by such act,
c) he commits such act for remuneration,
d) he commits such act repeatedly, or
e) he commits such act with the intent to conceal or facilitate another criminal offence. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years or forfeiture of property if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group and for remuneration,
b) he subjects a larger number of persons to inhuman or degrading treatment by such act,
c) he exposes another to danger of death by such act,
d) he causes serious bodily harm by such act,
e) he obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another by such act,
f) he commits such act with a weapon, or
g) he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years, or in addition to this punishment also by forfeiture of property, if
a) he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons by such act,
c) he obtains a large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act, or
d) he commits such act as a soldier during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Aiding Unauthorised Residence in the Territory of the Republic
(1) Whoever, with the intent to obtain an unlawful material or other benefit, aids another in unauthorised residence in the territory of the Czech Republic shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years if
a) he organises the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) he commits such act as a member of an organised group,
c) he commits such act repeatedly, or
d) he commits such act with the intent to conceal or facilitate another criminal offence. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, forfeiture of property or a fine if
a) he obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years, or in addition to this punishment also by forfeiture of property, if
a) he obtains a large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or
b) he commits such act as a soldier during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Common Provision
Protection under §§ 339 to 341 shall also be provided to a state which is a Member State of the European Union or applies the Schengen Borders Code in full. Unauthorised Employment of Foreigners
(1) Whoever systematically, repeatedly, under particularly exploitative working conditions or on a larger scale unlawfully employs or arranges employment for a foreigner who is residing unlawfully in the territory of the Czech Republic or does not have a valid work permit, if required under another legal regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, forfeiture of a thing or prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever employs or arranges employment for a foreigner who is a child and is residing unlawfully in the territory of the Czech Republic or does not have a valid work permit under another legal regulation. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2
a) as a member of an organised group,
(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years, or in addition to this punishment also by forfeiture of property, if he obtains a large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. Violation of Regulations on International Flights
