Whoever violates laws on international flights by entering the territory of the Czech Republic by air transport shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. Mutiny of Prisoners
(1) Whoever participates in the defiance or resistance of a group of prisoners or persons in the execution of security detention against a supervisory authority, its order, prison regulations or regulations on the execution of security detention shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if
a) he organises the act referred to in paragraph 1,
b) he commits such act with a weapon,
c) he commits such act with the intent to conceal or facilitate another criminal offence,
d) he causes serious bodily harm by such act,
e) he causes substantial damage by such act, or
f) he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1
b) serious bodily harm to at least two persons, or
False Accusation
(1) Whoever falsely accuses another of a criminal offence shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) Whoever falsely accuses another of a criminal offence with the intent to bring about his criminal prosecution shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 with the intent to obtain a benefit for himself or another,
b) he commits such act by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means,
c) he commits such act with the intent to seriously harm another in employment, disrupt his family relations or cause him other serious harm,
d) he commits such act with the intent to conceal or mitigate his own criminal offence, or
e) he commits such act against another who was fulfilling his duty towards him arising from his employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him by law. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) he causes substantial damage by such act, or
c) he obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another by such act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if
a) he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) he obtains a large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act. False Testimony and False Expert Opinion
(1) Whoever, as an expert, submits a false, grossly distorted or incomplete expert opinion shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever, as a witness or expert, before a court or before an international judicial body, before a notary as a judicial commissioner, a public prosecutor or before a police authority conducting preparatory proceedings under the Criminal Procedure Code, or before an investigative commission of the Chamber of Deputies of the Parliament of the Czech Republic
a) states an untruth about a circumstance that is of substantial significance for a decision or for the findings of an investigative commission of the Chamber of Deputies of the Parliament of the Czech Republic, or
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or prohibition of activity. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 with the intent to obtain a benefit for himself or another, or
b) he commits such act with the intent to seriously harm another in employment, disrupt his family relations or cause him other serious harm. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) he causes substantial damage by such act, or
c) he obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another by such act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if
a) he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) he obtains a large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act. False Interpretation
(1) Whoever, as an interpreter, incorrectly, grossly distortedly or incompletely interprets or translates in writing in proceedings before a public authority or in connection with such proceedings facts or circumstances that are of substantial significance for the decision of the public authority shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever, as an interpreter, before a court or before an international judicial body, before a notary as a judicial commissioner, a public prosecutor or before a police authority conducting preparatory proceedings under the Criminal Procedure Code, or before an investigative commission of the Chamber of Deputies of the Parliament of the Czech Republic incorrectly, grossly distortedly or incompletely interprets or translates in writing shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or prohibition of activity. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 with the intent to obtain a benefit for himself or another, or
b) he commits such act with the intent to seriously harm another in employment, disrupt his family relations or cause him other serious harm. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) he causes substantial damage by such act, or
c) he obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another by such act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if
a) he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) he obtains a large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act. Obstruction of Justice
(1) Whoever, for the purposes of initiating proceedings before a court, before an international judicial body or criminal proceedings or in such proceedings, presents material or documentary evidence that is of substantial significance for a decision, which he knows to be forged or altered, with the intent that it be used as genuine, or forges or alters such evidence with the intent that it be used as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) Whoever, himself or through another, provides, offers or promises a benefit to another or for another for the purpose of committing the criminal offence of false accusation (§ 345), false testimony and false expert opinion (§ 346) or false interpretation (§ 347) shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 with the intent to obtain a benefit for himself or another,
b) he commits such act as an official person,
c) he commits such act with the intent to seriously harm another in employment, disrupt his family relations or cause him other serious harm, or
d) he commits such act against another who was fulfilling his duty towards him arising from his employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him by law. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,
b) he causes substantial damage by such act, or
c) he obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another by such act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if
a) he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) he obtains a large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act. Forgery and Alteration of a Public Document
(1) Whoever forges a public document or substantially alters its content with the intent that it be used as genuine, or uses such document as genuine,
whoever obtains such document for himself or another or keeps it with the intent that it be used as genuine, or
whoever manufactures, offers, sells, arranges or otherwise makes available, obtains for himself or another or keeps an instrument, device or its component, procedure, aid or any other means, including a computer program, created or adapted for forging or altering a public document,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,
b) he causes substantial damage by such act, or
c) he obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another by such act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group operating in multiple states,
b) he commits such act with the intent to enable or facilitate the commission of a terrorist criminal offence, the criminal offence of participation in a terrorist group (§ 312a), financing of terrorism (§ 312d), support and promotion of terrorism under § 312e paragraph 3 or threatening with a terrorist criminal offence (§ 312f),
c) he causes large-scale damage by such act, or
d) he obtains a large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act. Unauthorised Production and Possession of a Seal of State Seal and Official Stamp
Whoever unlawfully manufactures, obtains for himself or another or keeps a seal of state seal or a stamp of a public authority with the depiction of the state emblem or a stamp whose imprint is a mandatory component of public documents, or an object capable of fulfilling their function, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or prohibition of activity. Forgery and Issuance of False Medical Report, Opinion and Finding
(1) Whoever forges a medical report, opinion or finding or substantially alters their content with the intent to use them in proceedings before a social security authority or before another public authority, in criminal, civil or other judicial proceedings, or
whoever uses such report, opinion or finding as genuine in proceedings before a social security authority or before another public authority, in criminal, civil or other judicial proceedings,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity. whoever, as a physician or other qualified health professional, issues a false or grossly distorted medical report, opinion or finding or conceals substantial facts therein about his own or another's health condition, so that it may be used in proceedings before a social security authority or before another public authority, in criminal, civil or other judicial proceedings, or
whoever uses such medical report, opinion or finding in proceedings before a social security authority or before another public authority, in criminal, civil or other judicial proceedings. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or a fine if
a) he obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) he causes substantial damage by such act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he obtains a large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or
b) he causes large-scale damage by such act. Obstruction of Preparation and Conduct of Elections and Referendum
Whoever prevents another by violence or threat of violence or by deceit from exercising the right to vote or the right to vote in a referendum or forces another in such manner to exercise the right to vote or the right to vote in a referendum,
whoever provides, offers or promises to another or for another in connection with the exercise of the right to vote or the right to vote in a referendum a financial, material or other similar benefit, so that he votes or votes contrary to the independent expression of his will,
whoever forges data in a document on the number of members of a political party or on a petition for electoral purposes or in another document related to elections or a referendum or knowingly uses such document as genuine,
whoever knowingly incorrectly counts votes or violates the secrecy of voting, or
whoever otherwise grossly obstructs the preparation or conduct of elections to a legislative body or a representative body of a territorial self-governing unit or the preparation or conduct of a referendum, until the announcement of their results,
shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. Criminal Offences Disrupting the Coexistence of People
Violence Against a Group of Inhabitants and Against an Individual
(1) Whoever threatens a group of inhabitants with death, bodily harm or causing large-scale damage shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) Whoever uses violence against a group of inhabitants or an individual or threatens them with death, bodily harm or causing large-scale damage because of their actual or supposed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because they are actually or supposedly without religion shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. (3) The same punishment as in paragraph 2 shall be imposed on
a) whoever conspires or assembles to commit such act, or
b) whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means. Dangerous Threatening
(1) Whoever threatens another with death, serious bodily harm or other serious harm in such manner that it may arouse justified apprehension shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or prohibition of activity if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) as a member of an organised group,
b) against a child or a pregnant woman,
d) against a witness, expert or interpreter in connection with the performance of their duty, or
e) against a health professional in the performance of health employment or profession aimed at saving life or protecting health or against another who was fulfilling his similar duty in the protection of life, health or property arising from his employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him by law. Dangerous Stalking
(1) Whoever persistently stalks another by
a) threatening him with bodily harm or other harm to him or his close persons,
b) seeking his personal proximity or following him,
c) persistently contacting him by means of electronic communications, in writing or otherwise,
d) restricting him in his usual way of life, or
e) misusing his personal data for the purpose of obtaining personal or other contact,
and this conduct is capable of arousing in him justified apprehension for his life or health or for the life and health of persons close to him, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) against a child or a pregnant woman,
Defamation of Nation, Race, Ethnic or Other Group of Persons
a) a nation, its language, a race or ethnic group, or
b) a group of persons because of their actual or supposed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because they are actually or supposedly without religion,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) with at least two persons, or
b) by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means. Incitement to Hatred Against a Group of Persons or to Restriction of Their Rights and Freedoms
(1) Whoever publicly incites hatred against a nation, race, ethnic group, religion, class or other group of persons or to restriction of the rights and freedoms of their members shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever conspires or assembles to commit the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means, or
b) he actively participates by such act in the activity of a group, organisation or association that proclaims discrimination, violence or racial, ethnic, class, religious or other hatred. Other Disturbances of Public Order
Spreading Alarming News
(1) Whoever intentionally causes danger of serious alarm to at least part of the population of a place by spreading alarming news that is false shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever communicates the news referred to in paragraph 1 or other false news that is capable of causing measures leading to danger of serious alarm to at least part of the population of a place or unjustified rescue work of the integrated rescue system to a court, an authority of the Police of the Czech Republic, an authority of state administration, territorial self-government, or another public authority, a legal person, a natural person who is an entrepreneur, or a mass information medium shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or prohibition of activity. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 repeatedly,
b) he commits such act as a member of an organised group,
c) he causes substantial damage by such act,
d) he causes serious disruption in the activity of an authority of state administration, territorial self-government, a court or another public authority by such act, or
e) he causes serious disruption in the activity of a legal or natural person who is an entrepreneur by such act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if
a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening the life or health of people, public order or property, or
b) he causes large-scale damage by such act. Disorderly Conduct
(1) Whoever commits publicly or in a place accessible to the public gross indecency or disorderly conduct, in particular by attacking another, defiling a grave, a historical or cultural monument, or grossly disrupting the preparation, conduct or conclusion of an organised sporting match, assembly or ceremony of people, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
b) as a member of an organised group. Defiling Human Remains
(1) Whoever unlawfully opens a grave or tomb or an urn with human remains shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever arbitrarily removes human remains from a burial ground or handles human remains in violation of the law. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2
a) as a member of an organised group,
b) with the intent to obtain a material benefit for himself or another, or
c) with the intent to conceal or facilitate another criminal offence. Intoxication
(1) Whoever, by consumption or application of an addictive substance, brings himself, even through negligence, into a state of insanity in which he commits an otherwise criminal act shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years; however, if he commits an otherwise criminal act for which the law prescribes a milder punishment, he shall be punished by this milder punishment. (2) The provision of paragraph 1, as well as § 26, shall not apply if the offender brought himself into a state of insanity with the intent to commit a criminal offence, or committed a criminal offence through negligence consisting in bringing himself into a state of insanity. Organised Criminal Group
Participation in an Organised Criminal Group
(1) Whoever establishes an organised criminal group,
whoever participates in the activity of an organised criminal group, or
whoever supports an organised criminal group,
shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years or forfeiture of property. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years or forfeiture of property if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a leading functionary or representative of an organised criminal group. (3) The provisions of §§ 107 and 108 shall not apply to the offender of the act referred to in paragraphs 1 and 2. Special Provision on Effective Remorse
Whoever commits the act referred to in § 361 shall not be punishable if he made a report about the organised criminal group to a public prosecutor or a police authority at a time when the danger that arose to an interest protected by this Act from another act of the organised criminal group, other than that referred to in § 361, could still be removed. A soldier may make such report also to a superior. Impunity of Agent
(1) An agent who participates in the activity of an organised criminal group or supports an organised criminal group shall not be punishable for the criminal offence of participation in an organised criminal group under § 361 if he committed such act with the aim of detecting the offender of criminal activity committed for the benefit of the organised criminal group or preventing its commission. (2) An agent who participates in the activity of an organised criminal group or as a member of an organised group or supports such group shall not be punishable for the criminal offences referred to in § 312c paragraph 2 if he committed such act with the aim of detecting the offender of criminal activity committed by members of the organised group, in connection with the organised group or committed for the benefit of the organised criminal group or preventing its commission. (3) An agent who established or masterminded the organised criminal group or the organised group or an agent who is a leading functionary or representative of the organised criminal group shall not become unpunishable. Certain Other Forms of Criminal Complicity
Incitement to a Criminal Offence
Whoever publicly incites to a criminal offence shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. Approval of a Criminal Offence
(1) Whoever publicly approves a committed felony or whoever publicly praises its offender for the felony shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, with the intent to express consent with a criminal offence
a) rewards or compensates the offender or a person close to him for the punishment, or
b) organises a collection for such reward or compensation. Harbouring
(1) Whoever assists the offender of a criminal offence with the intent to enable him to escape criminal prosecution, punishment or protective measure or their execution shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years; however, if he thus assists the offender of a criminal offence for which the criminal law prescribes a milder punishment, he shall be punished by that milder punishment. (2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 for the benefit of a person close to him shall not be punishable, unless he did so with the intent to
a) assist a person who committed the criminal offence of high treason (§ 309), subversion of the republic (§ 310), terrorist attack (§ 311), terror (§ 312), participation in a terrorist group (§ 312a), financing of terrorism (§ 312d), support and promotion of terrorism (§ 312e), threatening with a terrorist criminal offence (§ 312f), genocide (§ 400), attack against humanity (§ 401), apartheid and discrimination of a group of people (§ 402), aggression (§ 405a), preparation of an offensive war (§ 406), use of a prohibited means of combat and unlawful conduct of combat (§ 411), war cruelty (§ 412), persecution of the population (§ 413), looting in the area of war operations (§ 414) or misuse of internationally recognised and state symbols (§ 415), or
b) obtain a material benefit for himself or another. Failure to Prevent a Criminal Offence
(1) Whoever learns in a credible manner that another is preparing or committing the criminal offence of murder (§ 140), manslaughter (§ 141), serious bodily harm (§ 145), torture and other inhuman and cruel treatment (§ 149), unlawful termination of pregnancy without the consent of the pregnant woman (§ 159), unauthorised removal of tissues and organs (§ 164), trafficking in human beings (§ 168), deprivation of personal liberty (§ 170), abduction under § 172 paragraphs 2 and 3, robbery (§ 173), hostage taking (§ 174), extortion under § 175 paragraphs 3 and 4, unauthorised handling of personal data under § 180 paragraph 4, rape (§ 185), sexual abuse (§ 187), abuse of a child for the production of pornography (§ 193), abuse of a person entrusted to one's care (§ 198), theft under § 205 paragraph 5, embezzlement under § 206 paragraph 5, fraud under § 209 paragraph 5, insurance fraud under § 210 paragraph 6, credit fraud under § 211 paragraph 6, subsidy fraud under § 212 paragraph 6, legalisation of proceeds of crime under § 216 paragraphs 4 and 5, forgery and alteration of money (§ 233), unauthorised procurement, forgery and alteration of a payment instrument (§ 234), unauthorised production of money (§ 237), misuse of information in business dealings under § 255 paragraph 4, abuse of position in business dealings under § 255a paragraph 3, damage to the financial interests of the European Union under § 260 paragraph 5, violation of regulations on control of export of goods and dual-use technologies (§ 262), breach of obligations in the export of goods and dual-use technologies (§ 263), conducting foreign trade in military material without a permit or licence (§ 265), breach of obligation in connection with the issuance of a permit and licence for foreign trade in military material (§ 266), general endangerment (§ 272), development, production and possession of prohibited means of combat (§ 280), unauthorised production and possession of radioactive substance and highly dangerous substance (§ 281), unauthorised production and possession of nuclear material and special fissile material (§ 282), unauthorised production and other handling of narcotic and psychotropic substances and poisons (§ 283), seizure of control over an aircraft, civilian vessel and fixed platform (§ 290), abduction of an aircraft abroad (§ 292), high treason (§ 309), subversion of the republic (§ 310), terrorist attack (§ 311), terror (§ 312), participation in a terrorist group (§ 312a), financing of terrorism (§ 312d), support and promotion of terrorism (§ 312e), threatening with a terrorist criminal offence (§ 312f), sabotage (§ 314), espionage (§ 316), endangering classified information (§ 317), war treason (§ 320), violence against a public authority under § 323 paragraphs 3 and 4, violence against an official person under § 325 paragraphs 3 and 4, acceptance of a bribe (§ 331), bribery (§ 332), violent crossing of the state border under § 339 paragraphs 2 and 3, organising and enabling unlawful crossing of the state border under § 340 paragraph 4, mutiny of prisoners (§ 344), disobedience of an order under § 375 paragraphs 2 and 3, insubordination and coercion to breach military duty under § 377 paragraphs 2 and 3, violation of rights and protected interests of soldiers of equal rank under § 382 paragraphs 3 and 4, violation of rights and protected interests of subordinate soldiers or soldiers of lower rank under § 383 paragraphs 3 and 4, desertion (§ 386), endangering the moral state of soldiers under § 392 paragraph 2, genocide (§ 400), attack against humanity (§ 401), apartheid and discrimination of a group of people (§ 402), aggression (§ 405a), preparation of an offensive war (§ 406), contacts endangering peace (§ 409), use of a prohibited means of combat and unlawful conduct of combat (§ 411), war cruelty (§ 412), persecution of the population (§ 413), looting in the area of war operations (§ 414) or misuse of internationally recognised and state symbols under § 415 paragraph 3, and does not prevent the commission or completion of such criminal offence, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years; if this Act prescribes a milder punishment for any of these criminal offences, he shall be punished by that milder punishment. (2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall not be punishable if he could not prevent the criminal offence without considerable difficulties or without exposing himself or a person close to him to danger of death, bodily harm, other serious harm or criminal prosecution. However, exposing a person close to him to danger of criminal prosecution does not relieve the offender of criminal liability if the failure to prevent the criminal offence concerns high treason (§ 309), subversion of the republic (§ 310), terrorist attack (§ 311), terror (§ 312), participation in a terrorist group (§ 312a), financing of terrorism (§ 312d), support and promotion of terrorism (§ 312e), threatening with a terrorist criminal offence (§ 312f), sabotage (§ 314), espionage (§ 316), genocide (§ 400), attack against humanity (§ 401), apartheid and discrimination of a group of people (§ 402), aggression (§ 405a), preparation of an offensive war (§ 406), use of a prohibited means of combat and unlawful conduct of combat (§ 411), war cruelty (§ 412), persecution of the population (§ 413), looting in the area of war operations (§ 414) and misuse of internationally recognised and state symbols under § 415 paragraph 3. (3) A criminal offence may also be prevented by its timely report to a public prosecutor or a police authority; a soldier may instead make the report to a superior.Failure to Report a Criminal Offence
(1) Whoever learns in a credible manner that another person has committed the criminal offence of murder (§ 140), grievous bodily harm (§ 145), torture and other inhuman and cruel treatment (§ 149), trafficking in human beings (§ 168), deprivation of personal liberty (§ 170), hostage taking (§ 174), abuse of a child for the production of pornography (§ 193), ill-treatment of a person entrusted to one's care (§ 198), counterfeiting and alteration of money (§ 233), unauthorised procurement, counterfeiting and alteration of a means of payment (§ 234), unauthorised production of money (§ 237), breach of regulations on the control of export of dual-use goods and technologies (§ 262), breach of obligations in the export of dual-use goods and technologies (§ 263), conducting foreign trade in military material without authorisation or licence (§ 265), breach of obligation in connection with the issue of authorisation and licence for foreign trade in military material (§ 266), general endangerment (§ 272), development, production and possession of prohibited means of combat (§ 280), unauthorised production and possession of radioactive substance and highly dangerous substance (§ 281), unauthorised production and possession of nuclear material and special fissile material (§ 282), seizure of control of an aircraft, civilian vessel and fixed platform (§ 290), diversion of an aircraft abroad (§ 292), high treason (§ 309), subversion of the republic (§ 310), terrorist attack (§ 311), terror (§ 312), participation in a terrorist group (§ 312a), financing of terrorism (§ 312d), support and promotion of terrorism (§ 312e), sabotage (§ 314), espionage (§ 316), endangering classified information (§ 317), war treason (§ 320), acceptance of a bribe (§ 331), bribery (§ 332), genocide (§ 400), attack against humanity (§ 401), apartheid and discrimination of a group of people (§ 402), aggression (§ 405a), preparation of an aggressive war (§ 406), use of a prohibited means of combat and unlawful conduct of combat (§ 411), war cruelty (§ 412), persecution of the population (§ 413), pillaging in the area of war operations (§ 414) or abuse of internationally recognised and state symbols under § 415 para. 3, and fails to report such criminal offence without delay to a public prosecutor or a police authority or instead, in the case of a soldier, to a superior, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years; where this Act provides for a milder penalty for any of these criminal offences, he shall be punished by that milder penalty. (2) A person who commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall not be punishable if he could not make the report without exposing himself or a person close to him to the danger of death, bodily harm, other serious detriment or criminal prosecution. (3) The reporting obligation under paragraph 1 shall not apply to an advocate or his employee who learns of the commission of a criminal offence in connection with the practice of advocacy or legal practice. The reporting obligation shall also not apply to a clergyman of a registered church and religious society with authorisation to exercise special rights if he learns of the commission of a criminal offence in connection with the exercise of the seal of confession or in connection with the exercise of a right analogous to the seal of confession. The reporting obligation for the criminal offence of trafficking in human beings under § 168 para. 2 and deprivation of personal liberty (§ 170) shall also not apply to a person providing assistance to victims of criminal offences. CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST DEFENCE OBLIGATIONS
Frustrating Fitness for Service
(1) Whoever renders himself or another person permanently or temporarily unfit or less fit to fulfil defence obligations in peacetime shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Failure to Fulfil Conscription Obligation
(1) Whoever fails to appear for conscription proceedings with the intention of not fulfilling his conscription obligation or delaying its fulfilment shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Evasion of Defence Obligation
(1) Whoever engages in machinations with the intention of obtaining relief from the fulfilment of defence obligations shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) Whoever engages in machinations with the intention of
a) evading the fulfilment of defence obligations wholly or in part himself, or
b) exempting another person wholly or in part from the fulfilment of defence obligations,
shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to two years. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Failure to Report for Service in the Armed Forces
Whoever, on the basis of a call-up order duly served on him, fails to report for service in the armed forces during a state of emergency within 24 hours after the expiry of the time limit specified in such call-up order, although he was not prevented from doing so by any obstacle provided for by law, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. Failure to Report for Extraordinary Service in the Armed Forces
(1) Whoever, on the basis of a call-up order duly served on him, fails to report for extraordinary service in the armed forces during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war within 24 hours after the expiry of the time limit specified in such call-up order, although he was not prevented from doing so by any obstacle provided for by law, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. (2) Whoever, in the event that he does not receive a call-up order, fails to report for extraordinary service in the armed forces during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war on the basis of a public proclamation or mobilisation call, although he was not prevented from doing so by any obstacle provided for by law, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. Failure to Report for Extraordinary Service in the Armed Forces by Negligence
(1) Whoever, on the basis of a call-up order duly served on him, negligently fails to report for extraordinary service in the armed forces during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war within 24 hours after the expiry of the time limit specified in such call-up order, although he was not prevented from doing so by any obstacle provided for by law, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) Whoever, in the event that he does not receive a call-up order, negligently fails to report for extraordinary service in the armed forces during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war on the basis of a public proclamation or mobilisation call, although he was not prevented from doing so by any obstacle provided for by law, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. MILITARY CRIMINAL OFFENCES
Criminal Offences Against Military Subordination and Military Honour
Disobedience of an Order
(1) Whoever refuses to carry out or intentionally fails to carry out an order shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a group of soldiers,
b) commits such act with a weapon,
c) commits such act in circumstances where he could thereby frustrate or substantially impede the fulfilment of an important service task,
d) causes by such act endangerment of the combat readiness of a military unit, military facility, military rescue unit or other organisational unit within the competence of the Ministry of Defence,
e) causes by such act damage on a large scale or damage to the environment the removal of which requires expenditure at least on a large scale,
f) causes by such act serious harm to health, or
g) causes by such act death. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state, during a state of war or during a combat situation. Disobedience of an Order by Negligence
(1) Whoever negligently fails to carry out an order and thereby frustrates or substantially impedes the fulfilment of an important service task shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) serious endangerment of the combat readiness of a military unit, military facility, military rescue unit or other organisational unit within the competence of the Ministry of Defence,
b) damage on a large scale or damage to the environment the removal of which requires expenditure at least on a large scale,
c) serious harm to health, or
d) death. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state, during a state of war or during a combat situation. Resistance and Coercion to Breach Military Duty
(1) Whoever resists a soldier who is fulfilling a military duty or coerces him to breach a military duty shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a group of persons,
b) commits such act with a weapon,
c) commits such act during a combat situation, or
d) causes by such act serious harm to health. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he
a) causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 death, or
b) commits the act referred to in paragraph 2 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Insult Between Soldiers
(1) Whoever insults
a) a superior or a soldier of higher rank,
b) a subordinate or a soldier of lower rank, or
c) a soldier of the same rank at a time when he himself or the insulted person is performing guard, supervisory or other service,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 against another person on account of his actual or supposed race, membership of an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion. Insult Between Soldiers by Violence or Threat of Violence
(1) Whoever insults by violence or threat of imminent violence
a) a superior or a soldier of higher rank, or
b) a subordinate or a soldier of lower rank,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 at a time when he himself or the insulted person is performing guard, supervisory or other service,
b) commits such act against a military guard,
c) commits such act with a weapon or with at least two persons,
d) commits such act against another person on account of his actual or supposed race, membership of an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion, or
e) causes by such act bodily harm. Insult of a Soldier of the Same Rank by Violence or Threat of Violence
(1) Whoever insults by violence or threat of imminent violence a soldier of the same rank at a time when he himself or the insulted person is performing guard, supervisory or other service shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 against a military guard,
b) commits such act with a weapon or with at least two persons,
c) commits such act against another person on account of his actual or supposed race, membership of an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion, or
d) causes by such act bodily harm. Violence Against a Superior
(1) Whoever uses violence against a superior
a) with the intention of influencing the performance of his military duties, or
b) on account of the performance of his military duties,
shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he
a) causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 serious harm to health, or
b) commits such act with a weapon or with at least two persons. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he
a) causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 death, or
b) commits such act during a state of threat to the state, during a state of war or during a combat situation. Violation of the Rights and Protected Interests of Soldiers of the Same Rank
(1) Whoever compels a soldier of the same rank to perform personal services or restricts him in his rights or arbitrarily impedes the performance of his service shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by violence or threat of violence or threat of other serious harm,
b) commits such act with at least two persons,
c) commits such act against another person on account of his actual or supposed race, membership of an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion, or
d) causes by such act bodily harm. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a particularly brutal manner or with a weapon,
b) causes by such act serious harm to health, or
c) commits such act during a state of threat to the state, during a state of war or during a combat situation. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 death. Violation of the Rights and Protected Interests of Subordinate Soldiers or Soldiers of Lower Rank
(1) Whoever compels a subordinate soldier or a soldier of lower rank to perform personal services or restricts him in his rights or arbitrarily impedes the performance of his service or imposes on him a disciplinary punishment in breach of another legal regulation shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by violence or threat of violence or threat of other serious harm,
b) commits such act with at least two persons,
c) commits such act against another person on account of his actual or supposed race, membership of an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion, or
d) causes by such act bodily harm. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a particularly brutal manner or with a weapon,
b) causes by such act serious harm to health, or
c) commits such act during a state of threat to the state, during a state of war or during a combat situation. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 death. Criminal Offences Against the Duty to Perform Military Service
Evasion of Performance of Service
(1) Whoever during a state of emergency, with the intention of evading the performance of service, injures himself, feigns illness, forges a document, abuses an addictive substance or uses another ruse shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state, during a state of war or during a combat situation. Evasion of Performance of Service by Negligence
Whoever during a state of threat to the state, during a state of war or during a combat situation negligently renders himself unfit to perform service by abusing an addictive substance shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. Desertion
(1) Whoever absents himself without authorisation with the intention of evading military service shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Unauthorised Absence
(1) Whoever absents himself without authorisation by failing to appear in time for the departure or take-off of a military transport shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) Whoever absents himself without authorisation for a period longer than three days,
whoever absents himself without authorisation repeatedly for a period longer than 24 hours, although he has already been disciplinarily punished for such act in the last year,
whoever absents himself without authorisation for a period longer than two days during the performance of particularly important tasks, or
whoever, while serving abroad, absents himself without authorisation for a period longer than 24 hours,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (3) Whoever absents himself without authorisation for a period longer than fourteen days,
whoever absents himself without authorisation for a period longer than six days during the performance of particularly important tasks, or
whoever, while serving abroad, absents himself without authorisation for a period longer than three days,
shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. Criminal Offences Against the Duties of Guard, Supervisory or Other Service
Evasion of Performance of Guard, Supervisory or Other Service
(1) Whoever, with the intention of evading the performance of guard, supervisory or other service, injures himself, feigns illness, forges a document, abuses an addictive substance or uses another ruse shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to ten years or by an exceptional sentence if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state, during a state of war or during a combat situation. Breach of Duty of Guard Service
(1) Whoever in guard or other similar service breaches, even by negligence, the regulations or rules of such service or special orders issued pursuant thereto shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in the performance of guard service which has particularly important state or military significance,
b) commits such act by gross breach of his duty,
c) causes by such act a harmful consequence the prevention of which the performance of guard or other similar service was intended to achieve, or
d) commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he intentionally commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a combat situation and one of the circumstances referred to in paragraph 2 is present. Breach of Duty of Supervisory or Other Service
(1) Whoever in supervisory or other service, even by negligence, breaches the regulations or rules of such service shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 a particularly serious consequence which he was obliged to prevent. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a combat situation. Breach of Duty of Service in Air Defence
(1) Whoever breaches, even by negligence, the rules of service in facilities intended to ensure the security of airspace shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 a particularly serious consequence which he was obliged to prevent. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war and thereby causes a particularly serious consequence which he was obliged to prevent. Criminal Offences Endangering the Combat Capability of the Armed Forces
Endangering the Morale of Soldiers
(1) Whoever incites soldiers against military service or against a superior, or
whoever in a serious manner or systematically undermines military discipline,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war or during a combat situation. Breach of Service Duty of a Soldier
(1) Whoever, to the detriment of the armed forces, by failure to fulfil an imposed service duty according to his service assignment, even by negligence, substantially reduces the usability of military material shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever without authorisation uses military material of greater value for a purpose for which it is not intended or gives consent to such use, or
whoever abuses or enables the abuse of subordinates for tasks beyond the scope of service duties,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he
a) obtains for himself or another person considerable benefit by the act referred to in paragraph 2, or
b) causes by an intentionally committed act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 a reduction in the combat capability of the armed forces or considerable damage. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he causes by an intentionally committed act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 damage on a large scale. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he intentionally commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 during a combat situation, during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Cowardice Before the Enemy
Whoever during a combat situation surrenders into captivity out of cowardice or faint-heartedness shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twenty years. Failure to Fulfil a Combat Task
(1) Whoever absents himself without permission from the place of performance of service during a combat situation shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twenty years. (2) Whoever during a combat situation evades the duty to fulfil a combat task or refuses to use weapons shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years. Abandonment of Military Material
(1) Whoever during a combat situation discards, abandons or renders unusable military armament or other military equipment shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 a reduction in the combat capability of the armed forces or considerable damage. Surrender of Soldiers and Military Material to the Enemy
(1) A commander who, without it being necessitated by a combat situation, surrenders soldiers to the enemy or leaves him military armament, military equipment or technical facilities, even by negligence, shall be punished, unless he acted with the intention of supporting the enemy, by imprisonment for five to twenty years. Criminal Offences Against Service Duties of Members of Security Corps
Breach of Service Duty of a Member of a Security Corps
(1) Whoever, to the detriment of a security corps, by failure to fulfil an imposed service duty, even by negligence, substantially reduces the usability of armament, equipment or other material resources shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever without authorisation uses a material resource of greater value referred to in paragraph 1 for a purpose for which it is not intended or gives consent to such use, or
whoever abuses or enables the abuse of subordinates or inferiors for non-service acts,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he
a) obtains for himself or another person considerable benefit by the act referred to in paragraph 2, or
b) causes by an intentionally committed act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 considerable damage. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he causes by an intentionally committed act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 damage on a large scale. Common Provision
The provisions on the criminal offences of disobedience of an order (§ 375), disobedience of an order by negligence (§ 376), insult between soldiers (§ 378), insult between soldiers by violence or threat of violence (§ 379), insult of a soldier of the same rank by violence or threat of violence (§ 380), violence against a superior (§ 381), breach of duty of guard service (§ 389) and breach of duty of supervisory or other service (§ 390) shall also apply to members of a security corps. CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST HUMANITY, AGAINST PEACE AND WAR CRIMES
Criminal Offences Against Humanity
Genocide
(1) Whoever, with the intention of destroying in whole or in part any racial, ethnic, national, religious, class or other similar group of people,
a) places members of such group in such living conditions as are intended to bring about their complete or partial physical destruction,
b) implements measures aimed at preventing births within such group,
c) forcibly transfers children from one such group to another, or
d) causes serious harm to health or death to a member of such group,
shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever publicly incites the commission of the act referred to in paragraph 1. Attack Against Humanity
(1) Whoever, as part of a widespread or systematic attack directed against a civilian population, commits
a) murder,
b) extermination,
c) deportation or forcible transfer of a group of population,
d) rape, sexual slavery, enforced prostitution, forced pregnancy, enforced sterilisation or other similar form of sexual violence,
e) persecution of a group of population on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural or religious grounds, on the basis of gender or on other similar grounds,
f) apartheid or other similar segregation or discrimination,
g) deprivation of personal liberty, abduction to an unknown place or any other restriction of personal liberty resulting in the involuntary disappearance of persons,
h) enslavement,
i) torture,
j) another inhuman act of a similar nature,
shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence. Apartheid and Discrimination of a Group of People
(1) Whoever applies apartheid or racial, ethnic, national, religious or class segregation or other similar discrimination of a group of people shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he
a) plunges by the act referred to in paragraph 1 such group of people into harsh living conditions, or
b) subjects by such act such group of people to inhuman or degrading treatment. Establishment, Support and Promotion of a Movement Aimed at Suppressing Human Rights and Freedoms
(1) Whoever establishes, supports or promotes a movement which demonstrably aims at suppressing human rights and freedoms or professes racial, ethnic, national, religious or class hatred or hatred towards another group of persons shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means,
b) commits such act as a member of an organised group,
c) commits such act as a soldier, or
d) commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Dissemination of Work for the Promotion of a Movement Aimed at Suppressing Human Rights and Freedoms
(1) Whoever on a larger scale produces, imports, exports, transports, offers, makes publicly accessible, mediates, puts into circulation, sells or otherwise procures for another or creates for another a work which depicts, captures or otherwise represents symbols, in particular logos, flags, badges, uniforms and their parts, slogans, statements, declarations, slogans and forms of greetings, representatives or manifestations of representatives of the movement referred to in § 403 para. 1 shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, a fine or forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) as a member of an organised group,
b) by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means, or
c) with the intention of obtaining considerable benefit for himself or another person. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years or by a fine if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with the intention of obtaining benefit on a large scale for himself or another person. Special Provision on Impunity
Under § 403a, a person who produces, imports, exports, transports, offers, makes publicly accessible, mediates, puts into circulation, sells or otherwise procures for another or creates for another such work for the purpose of education, research, art, reporting on current or historical events or for similar purposes shall not be punishable. Expression of Sympathy for a Movement Aimed at Suppressing Human Rights and Freedoms
Whoever publicly expresses sympathy for the movement referred to in § 403 para. 1 shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. Denial, Questioning, Approval and Justification of Genocide
Whoever publicly denies, questions, approves or attempts to justify Nazi, Communist or other genocide or Nazi, Communist or other crimes against humanity or war crimes or crimes against peace shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. Criminal Offences Against Peace and War Crimes
Aggression
Whoever, in a position which enables him to exercise control over a state or to direct its political or military actions, in breach of the provisions of international law plans, prepares, initiates or carries out an act of aggression which consists in the use of armed force by such state against the sovereignty, territorial integrity or political independence of another state or in the use of armed force by such state in any other manner inconsistent with the Charter of the United Nations and which by its character, gravity and scale constitutes a manifest violation of the Charter of the United Nations shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence. Preparation of an Aggressive War
Whoever prepares an aggressive war in which the Czech Republic is to participate and thereby brings about for the Czech Republic the danger of war shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence. Incitement to an Aggressive War
(1) Whoever publicly incites an aggressive war in which the Czech Republic is to participate, promotes such war or otherwise supports war propaganda shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 at an assembly which is subject to a notification obligation, or
b) commits such act by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means. Common Provision
Preparation and incitement to an aggressive war shall not include conduct connected with
a) the declaration of a state of war if there is a threat that the Czech Republic will be attacked or if it is necessary to fulfil international treaty obligations on collective defence against attack,
b) the participation of the Czech Republic in defence systems of an international organisation of which the Czech Republic is a member,
c) the dispatch of armed forces of the Czech Republic outside the territory of the Czech Republic or the stay of armed forces of other states on the territory of the Czech Republic to which the Parliament of the Czech Republic or the Government of the Czech Republic gives consent. Contacts Endangering Peace
(1) A citizen of the Czech Republic or a stateless person who has been granted permanent residence on its territory who, with the intention of bringing about war or armed action against the Czech Republic or another state, himself or through another person establishes or maintains contacts with a foreign power shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1
a) serious endangerment of the international standing of the Czech Republic or another state, or
b) serious endangerment of the existence of the Czech Republic or another state. Breach of International Sanctions
(1) Whoever on a larger scale breaches a command, prohibition or restriction established for the purpose of maintaining or restoring international peace and security, protecting human rights and freedoms, combating terrorism, observing international law, supporting democracy and the rule of law, to the observance of which the Czech Republic is bound by its membership in the United Nations or in the European Union or which the Czech Republic has introduced pursuant to the sanctions law shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by a fine. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he
a) causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1 considerable damage, or
b) obtains by such act considerable benefit for himself or another person. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he
a) commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in connection with an organised group operating in more than one state,
b) commits such act with the intention of obtaining benefit on a large scale for himself or another person,
c) causes by such act damage on a large scale,
d) causes by such act serious endangerment of the international standing of the Czech Republic, or
e) contributes by such act substantially to the disruption of international peace and security, measures aimed at protecting human rights and freedoms, combating terrorism, observing international law or supporting democracy and the rule of law.Use of prohibited means of combat and unlawful conduct of combat
(1) Whoever during war or other armed conflict or in a combat situation
a) orders the use of prohibited means of combat or material of a similar nature or uses such means or material, or
b) orders the conduct of combat by prohibited methods or conducts combat in such manner himself,
shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, in violation of the provisions of international law on the means and methods of conducting war or other armed conflict, intentionally
a) causes harm to civilian population or civilians in their life, health or property by military operation or conducts an attack against them for reasons of reprisals,
b) conducts an attack against an undefended place or demilitarized zone,
c) destroys or damages a dam, nuclear power plant or similar installation containing dangerous forces, or
d) destroys or damages an object designated for humanitarian purposes or an internationally recognized cultural or natural monument or abuses such object or monument for military purposes. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he causes by an act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2
a) serious bodily harm, or
War cruelty
(1) Whoever during war or other armed conflict violates the rules of international law by treating inhumanely civilian population, refugees, wounded, sick, members of armed forces who have already laid down their arms, or prisoners of war, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever during war or other armed conflict violates the rules of international law by
a) failing to implement effective measures for the protection of persons who need such assistance, especially children, women, wounded and sick, or preventing such measures, or
b) preventing or obstructing civil defence organizations of the enemy, neutral or other state in the performance of their humanitarian tasks. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he causes by an act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2
a) serious bodily harm, or
Persecution of population
(1) Whoever during war or other armed conflict applies apartheid or commits other inhumane acts arising from racial, ethnic, national, religious, class or other similar discrimination or terrorizes civilian population by violence or threat of its use, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever during war or other armed conflict
a) destroys or seriously disrupts a source of basic life necessities of civilian population in occupied territory or contact zone or arbitrarily fails to provide the population with assistance necessary for survival,
b) unreasonably delays the return of civilian population or prisoners of war,
c) unreasonably relocates or expels civilian population of occupied territory,
d) settles occupied territory with the population of his own country,
e) recruits children for service in arms, or
f) arbitrarily prevents civilian population or prisoners of war from having their offences decided in impartial judicial proceedings. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he causes by an act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2
a) serious bodily harm, or
Pillage in the area of war operations
(1) Whoever in the area of war operations, on the battlefield, in places affected by war operations, armed conflict or in occupied territory
a) robs the fallen or otherwise appropriates another's property, or
b) arbitrarily destroys, damages, takes away, conceals or abuses another's property,
shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence. Abuse of internationally recognized and state symbols
(1) Whoever during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war or during war or other armed conflict abuses the designation of the Red Cross or other distinctive signs or colours recognized by international law for the designation of medical institutions or means of transport for medical assistance or evacuation, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever during war or other armed conflict abuses the sign of the United Nations or the flag or state or military sign, insignia or uniform of a neutral or other state that is not a party to the conflict. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he causes by an act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2
a) death or serious bodily harm,
b) damage on a large scale, or
c) retaliatory measures by the other warring party or the other party to the armed conflict of the same or similar character. Abuse of flag and truce
(1) Whoever during war or other armed conflict abuses the flag or state or military sign, insignia or uniform of another state that is a party to the conflict, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. (2) Whoever during war or other armed conflict abuses a declared truce or flag of truce, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years. Injury to a parliamentarian
Whoever insults a parliamentarian or a member of his retinue, or
whoever unlawfully detains such person,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years. Common provision
Responsibility of a superior
(1) A military or other superior is criminally responsible for the criminal offence of genocide (§ 400), attack against humanity (§ 401), aggression (§ 405a), preparation of aggressive war (§ 406), incitement to aggressive war (§ 407), use of prohibited means of combat and unlawful conduct of combat (§ 411), war cruelty (§ 412), persecution of population (§ 413), pillage in the area of war operations (§ 414), abuse of internationally recognized and state symbols (§ 415), abuse of flag and truce (§ 416) or injury to a parliamentarian (§ 417) committed by his subordinate over whom he exercised his authority and control, if, even through negligence, he failed to prevent him from committing such criminal offence, failed to obstruct the commission of such criminal offence or failed to punish him for committing such criminal offence or failed to hand him over to an authority competent for such punishment. (2) The provisions on criminal responsibility and punishability of the subordinate offender shall apply to the criminal responsibility and punishability of a military or other superior. TRANSITIONAL AND FINAL PROVISIONS
Transitional provision
A sentence imposed before the date of entry into force of this Act for an act that is not a criminal offence under this Act, or its unserved remainder, shall not be executed. The provision on aggregate sentence shall not apply in such case. If an aggregate or cumulative sentence was imposed for such act and a concurrent criminal offence, the court shall proportionally reduce the sentence; in doing so, it shall take into account the mutual ratio of the seriousness of the acts that are not criminal offences from the date of entry into force of this Act and the concurrent criminal offences. Implementation of European Union law
This Act incorporates the relevant regulations of the European Union1). Repealing provision

Entry into force
This Act shall enter into force on 1 January 2010. Transitional provisions introduced by Act No. 357/2011 Coll. Art. IX
1. The provision of § 11 paragraph 2 of Act No. 40/2009 Coll., as amended with effect from the date of entry into force of this Act, does not apply to convictions by a court of another Member State of the European Union for a criminal offence committed before the date of entry into force of this Act. 2. The provision of § 11 paragraph 2 of Act No. 40/2009 Coll., as amended with effect from the date of entry into force of this Act, shall until 27 April 2012 apply only in relation to final convictions by a court of a Member State of the European Union recorded in the register of the Member State of which the convicted person is a citizen or in whose territory he has his habitual residence. Transitional provision introduced by Act No. 55/2017 Coll. Art. II
For the purposes of imposing the protective measure of confiscation of part of property pursuant to § 102a of Act No. 40/2009 Coll., as amended with effect from the date of entry into force of this Act, when determining the amount of gross disproportion between the value of property that the offender acquired or transferred to another person or into property in a trust fund or in a similar arrangement, and his income acquired in accordance with the law, only property that the offender acquired or transferred to another person or into property in a trust fund or in a similar arrangement from the date of entry into force of this Act may be taken into account. Transitional provisions introduced by Act No. 58/2017 Coll. Art. II
1. The placement of convicted persons for the execution of imprisonment in a prison with supervision and in a prison with surveillance shall be changed on the date of entry into force of this Act to placement in a prison with security. 2. When deciding on the manner of execution of unconditional imprisonment imposed for a criminal offence committed before the date of entry into force of this Act, § 56 of the Criminal Code, as amended with effect from the date of entry into force of this Act, shall be applied from the date of entry into force of this Act. 3. A motion for transfer to another basic type of prison, which was not finally decided before the date of entry into force of this Act, shall not be taken into account, unless it is a motion for transfer from a prison with enhanced security to a prison with security or a motion for transfer from a prison with security to a prison with enhanced security. Transitional provision introduced by Act No. 333/2020 Coll. Art. II
In proceedings commenced before the date of entry into force of this Act, § 138 paragraph 1 of Act No. 40/2009 Coll., as amended until the date of entry into force of this Act, shall be used for the purposes of determining subject-matter jurisdiction. Footnotes
